To assess robustness under domain shifts (e.g., night-time captures, occlusion, motion-blur), we extend our evaluation across three independent reduced-bias test sets (including ENA-Detection, Caltech Camera Traps and Missouri Camera Traps). Across these out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluations, our LTR-enhanced model consistently demonstrates substantially stronger generalisation capabilities compared to standard cross-entropy approaches. However, qualitative and quantitative analyses underline that current LTR optimisations cannot fully overcome representational bottlenecks, resulting in catastrophic predictive breakdown for rare `Tail' classes under severe domain shift. For maximum reproducibility, all dataset splits, key code, and network weights are published with this paper at https://github.com/ZehuaLiuY/Species-Classification.

</details>


### [173] [When 2D Cues Fail: Improving Image Manipulation Localization with Reliable 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18040)
*Guofeng Yu,Zhiqing Guo,Dan Ma,Gaobo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的图像篡改定位框架（GFrame），通过单目重建获取深度和表面法线等几何线索，并选择性利用可靠几何信息与RGB特征融合，以解决传统2D取证线索在篡改区域与背景高度融合时失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IML方法依赖2D取证线索（如低层伪影、噪声痕迹、语义不一致），但当篡改区域与背景外观高度融合时，这些线索区分度显著下降。然而篡改区域虽在局部外观上一致，却可能违反场景的几何结构，因此需要引入几何推理来弥补2D证据的不足。

Method: 利用单目重建获取深度和表面法线作为辅助几何线索；不直接使用噪声较大的重建几何，而是估计其可靠性并选择性利用；设计GFrame框架，将可靠几何线索与RGB特征融合，并在多尺度间传播以提升细粒度定位。

Result: 在有限预算约束下，所提方法取得了优越的性能，表明可靠的3D几何能为IML提供超越传统2D线索的互补取证证据。

Conclusion: 引入几何推理并选择性利用可靠几何线索，是解决2D取证在高度融合场景下失效问题的有效途径，3D几何信息可作为图像篡改定位的重要补充证据。

Abstract: Existing image manipulation localization (IML) methods rely heavily on 2D forensic cues, such as low-level artifacts, noise traces, and semantic inconsistencies in the manipulated image. While effective in many cases, these cues become much less discriminative when manipulated regions are well blended with their surrounding context in appearance. In such cases, a manipulated region may remain locally appearance-consistent, but still violate the geometric structure of the surrounding scene. This limitation motivates us to go beyond purely 2D evidence and introduce geometric reasoning into IML. To this end, we leverage monocular reconstruction to obtain auxiliary geometric cues, including depth and surface normals. However, a key challenge lies in the fact that reconstructed geometry on manipulated images is inherently noisy and cannot be used naively. Rather than treating depth and normals as direct evidence, we estimate their reliability and exploit them selectively for localization. Based on this principle, we design a geometry-aware framework (GFrame) that fuses reliable geometric cues with RGB features and propagates them across scales to improve fine-grained localization. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method achieves excellent performance under limited budget constraints. These results indicate that reliable 3D geometry provides complementary forensic evidence beyond traditional 2D cues for IML. Related code will be released.

</details>


### [174] [Anticipate Before Acting: Future-State-Conditioned Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18042)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Zhanguang Zhang,Liheng Ma,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出FSC-VLN方法，通过在训练时引入未来视觉隐变量作为监督信号，在不依赖测试时未来图像的情况下提升视觉-语言导航性能，在R2R数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆仅监督下一步动作，未显式训练策略状态预测未来视觉结果的能力。作者首先验证了未来观测信息对当前动作选择确实有帮助，进而提出如何在测试时无未来图像的情况下依然利用未来信息。

Method: 提出Future-State-Conditioned VLN (FSC-VLN)：在模型中添加未来查询token，通过仅在训练时存在的目标分支将其隐状态与冻结的Δ步后的视觉嵌入对齐，训练后移除该分支。采用双查询设计（分离未来查询和动作查询）。

Result: 在R2R val-unseen上，FSC-VLN在SR/OSR/SPL指标上优于StreamVLN风格基线，在长距离场景中增益更大，消融实验支持了双查询设计。

Conclusion: 通过训练时的未来视觉隐变量监督，可以有效提升VLN策略的决策质量，且不增加测试时推理成本。

Abstract: End-to-end vision-language navigation (VLN) with causal vision-language models can map instructions and egocentric observations directly to actions, but standard behavior cloning supervises only the next action and does not explicitly train the policy state to be predictive of future visual outcomes. We first ask a diagnostic question: if the policy is given an expert-trajectory future image as privileged input at training and testing time, is that additional visual evidence useful for choosing the current action? (These expert-trajectory future images are unavailable at test time in real deployment, so we use this setting only as a privileged-input diagnostic.) The answer is yes; this sanity check shows that future observations can provide rich, actionable cues.
  We then ask a deployable question: without accessing future images at inference, can we still benefit from future information by using a compressed future visual latent only as training supervision? We propose Future-State-Conditioned VLN (FSC-VLN), which adds a future-query token and aligns its hidden state to a frozen visual embedding $Δ$ steps ahead via a training-only target branch that is removed after training. On R2R val-unseen, FSC-VLN improves SR/OSR/SPL over a StreamVLN-style baseline under two training-data regimes, with larger gains on long-horizon episodes; ablations further support the dual-query design (separating future and action queries).

</details>


### [175] [Sparse Evidence Can Suffice: Agentic Evidence Seeking for Multimodal Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18080)
*Haochen Zhao,Yongxiu Xu,Xinkui Lin,Dong Xie,Jiarui Lu,Yuqi Qian,Yubin Wang,Hongbo Xu,Gaopeng Gou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIEVE框架，通过稀疏交互证据验证方法，将多模态视频虚假信息检测中的证据获取与验证解耦，利用证据搜寻代理主动采集关键线索，再交由验证器判断真伪。实验表明该方法在多个基准上优于现有方法，且提供了可解释的证据链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视频虚假信息检测方法通常采用整体视频理解的方式，但真实世界中的虚假信息往往呈现稀疏且组合式的证据结构——仅少数耦合线索就足以做出判断，大部分视频内容贡献有限。全量多模态推理会引入大量冗余并淹没关键证据，因此需要将证据获取与验证过程解耦。

Method: 提出SIEVE框架，包含一个证据搜寻代理（evidence-seeking agent）和一个验证器（verifier）。代理通过监督式证据搜寻轨迹训练，并采用证据感知的强化学习目标，鼓励获取有信息量的证据，同时抑制无效交互。代理主动探索多模态证据，构建紧凑的证据包，验证器基于此判断真伪。

Result: 在多个视频虚假信息检测基准上，SIEVE持续优于所有基线方法，能够使用紧凑的证据包实现可靠的验证。证据获取过程提供了明确、可检查的链条，提升了多模态虚假信息检测的透明性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 稀疏交互证据验证范式有效解决了多模态视频虚假信息检测中的冗余问题，既提升了检测性能，又增强了模型的可解释性和可信度。证据获取与验证的解耦设计为虚假信息检测提供了更合理的认知路径。

Abstract: Multimodal video misinformation detection is commonly formulated as a holistic video-understanding task, where the entire video and its associated content are processed and judged in a single pass. However, real-world misinformation often exhibits a sparse and compositional evidence structure: a reliable decision may depend on only a few coupled clues, while most video content contributes limited additional information. Exhaustive multimodal reasoning may therefore introduce substantial redundancy and obscure decisive evidence. This motivates decoupling evidence acquisition from verification: first identifying sparse, decision-relevant clues and then judging veracity based on the acquired evidence. Accordingly, we propose SIEVE, a framework for Sparse Interactive Evidence Verification via Extraction in multimodal video misinformation detection. An evidence-seeking agent actively explores the available multimodal evidence and constructs a compact evidence package, which is then used by a verifier to determine veracity. The agent is trained with supervised evidence-seeking trajectories and an evidence-aware reinforcement learning objective that promotes informative evidence acquisition while discouraging unnecessary or invalid interactions. Experiments on multiple video misinformation benchmarks show that SIEVE consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines and supports reliable verification using compact evidence packages. Moreover, the resulting acquisition process provides an explicit and inspectable evidence trail, improving the transparency and groundedness of multimodal misinformation detection.

</details>


### [176] [SAR Vessel Detection and Gross Tonnage Estimation from Heterogeneous Datasets for Dark Vessel Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18051)
*Davide Paltrinieri,Andrea Diecidue,Roberto Basla,Daniele Casciani,Piero Fraternali,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合多任务深度学习与KNN的框架，利用异构数据（SAR图像+表格数据）实现船舶检测与总吨位回归，从而识别关闭应答器的暗船。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR数据集缺乏总吨位标注，无法直接训练端到端模型来筛选需要携带应答器的船只，从而导致难以识别暗船。

Method: 多任务深度学习框架同时预测船舶位置、类型和物理尺寸，再级联非参数KNN模型（混合欧几里得+类别距离）基于尺寸和类别回归总吨位。

Result: 该方案在多项子任务上达到与现有最优模型相当的竞争力，能够有效识别暗船。

Conclusion: 通过异构数据融合与级联建模，成功突破了公开数据缺乏GT标注的限制，为暗船检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Detecting vessels engaging in illegal activities is of paramount importance for maritime security. One of the major goals is to detect dark vessels, ships that disable their transponders to evade surveillance. Deep Learning (DL) models can detect vessels in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) images, enabling maritime traffic analysis regardless of weather or visibility conditions. However, to detect potential dark vessels, a DL model must select only those that are required to carry a transponder based on their Gross Tonnage (GT). Unfortunately, no public SAR dataset is available for training an end-to-end DL model for vessel detection and GT regression. In this work, we present a framework that leverages heterogeneous image and tabular datasets to solve this task. Our solution combines a multi-task DL framework for predicting the location, vessel type, and physical dimensions of ships, cascaded with a non-parametric model for predicting GT from vessel size and category. We perform GT regression by a KNN that measures sample similarity using a hybrid Euclidean and categorical distance. Experiments show that our solution can predict multiple outputs while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art models on individual subtasks, thus enabling the identification of dark vessels. We publish our code on GitHub https://github.com/PaltrinieriDavide/vesseldetection.

</details>


### [177] [O-VAD: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection through Object-Centric Tracking and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18142)
*Mei Yuan,Qi Long,Qifeng Wu,Zhenyang Li,Yizhou Zhao,Lei Wang,Yang Liu,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、无需领域知识的智能体框架，通过追踪物体状态演化轨迹来检测工业视频中的异常，在三个数据集上超越前沿VLM和传统VAD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在工业场景中因物体变换复杂、物理约束严格而性能下降，且传统方法依赖重训练或领域知识注入，缺乏通用性。

Method: 构建无需训练的智能体框架，像人类检验员一样追踪物体的时空动态和状态演化，形成物体级时序轨迹，再推理识别异常。

Result: 在三个工业视频异常检测数据集上，该方法超越前沿VLM、其他智能体框架以及经过微调的传统VAD方法。

Conclusion: 该方法无需领域知识或重训练即可有效检测工业异常，并能提供可解释的异常过程和类型报告。

Abstract: Industrial Video Anomaly Detection (IVAD) aims to identify anomalous objects and events in an industrial process, which is crucial for modern manufacturing and quality control systems. Existing VLM-based anomaly reasoning methods are capable of detecting open-ended anomalies in general domains. However, their performance declines in industrial settings characterized by intricate object transformations, strict physics, and procedural constraints. To tackle the complexity of such interaction-intensive detection, we introduce a training-free agentic framework for anomaly detection free of domain-specific knowledge, emphasizing object state evolution like humans inspectors. It is designed to track spatial-temporal dynamics and underlying transformations of detected objects over time, and then reason over the object-wise temporal state trajectories to identify abnormal objects in grounded frames. Our method overcomes limitations of prior approaches that rely on retraining on normal clips or injecting domain knowledge as context for test-time inference. Extensive experiments on three IVAD datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms frontier VLMs, agentic frameworks, and traditional VAD methods fine-tuned on the respective datasets, while providing interpretable reports over anomaly processes and types.

</details>


### [178] [QIRF Quantum-Inspired Non-Orthogonal Function-Space Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18067)
*Shizeng Jiang,Hao Zhang,Xuerui Ma,Ying Hu,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QIRF是一种受量子启发的非正交函数空间压缩方法，通过将相邻高斯基元建模为局部非正交基，利用广义特征分解识别主导子空间并选择代表性高斯基元，在13个场景上平均减少71.7%的高斯数量和原始PLY存储（约3.54倍压缩），同时保持与3DGS相当的渲染质量并提升34.3%的渲染速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法主要通过逐基元剪枝、属性量化、聚类或神经编码来减少冗余，但尚未探索由强重叠和非正交高斯基函数引起的冗余。本文旨在挖掘这种未被重视的冗余来源。

Method: QIRF将相邻高斯基元建模为局部非正交基，构建解析高斯重叠矩阵和辐射响应密度矩阵来表征功能冗余和渲染相关性，通过广义特征分解识别主导局部子空间并选择代表性高斯基元，同时引入基于RRDM的响应模型和细节感知保护机制来保留重要高频结构。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples和Deep Blending的13个场景上，平均减少71.7%的高斯数量和原始PLY存储（约3.54倍压缩），PSNR平均提升0.10 dB，渲染速度提升34.3%。

Conclusion: 非正交函数空间冗余是显式高斯辐射场中一个重要但被忽视的表示冗余来源，QIRF有效利用了这一点实现了高效的压缩。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality real-time rendering by representing a scene with a large collection of anisotropic Gaussian primitives. However, complex scenes often require millions of Gaussians, resulting in substantial storage and rendering costs. Existing compression methods mainly reduce redundancy through primitive-wise pruning, attribute quantization, clustering, or neural coding, while redundancy caused by strongly overlapping and non-orthogonal Gaussian basis functions remains largely unexplored. We present QIRF, a quantum-inspired non-orthogonal function-space compression method for 3D Gaussian Splatting. QIRF models neighboring Gaussian primitives as a local non-orthogonal basis and formulates primitive reduction as a subspace-aware selection problem. Specifically, an analytic Gaussian overlap matrix and a radiance-response density matrix are constructed to characterize functional redundancy and rendering relevance. Generalized eigendecomposition is then used to identify the dominant local subspace and select representative Gaussian primitives. An RRDM-based response model and detail-aware safeguarding further preserve visually important high-frequency structures under aggressive pruning. Experiments on 13 scenes from Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending show that QIRF reduces the Gaussian count and raw PLY storage by 71.7 percent on average, corresponding to approximately 3.54 times compression, while maintaining reconstruction quality comparable to 3DGS and achieving a marginal average PSNR improvement of 0.10 dB. QIRF also improves the average rendering speed over 3DGS by 34.3 percent. These results suggest that non-orthogonal function-space redundancy is an important yet underexplored source of representational redundancy in explicit Gaussian radiance fields.

</details>


### [179] [VGOcc: Learning Visual-Geometric Gaussians for Vision-Centric 3D Driving Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18078)
*Junhong Lin,Xianda Guo,Kangli Wang,Yuqi Ye,Xiaoyu Liang,Yanlun Peng,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGOcc利用从基础模型中提取的视觉和几何线索，通过视觉-几何高斯初始化与姿态感知特征学习，提升纯视觉3D占据预测的精度，在nuScenes上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯原语的占据预测方法主要依赖图像域特征，缺乏足够的显式几何信息，难以有效支持三维体积推理。

Method: 1) 视觉-几何高斯诞生：从射线深度假设中形成空间平衡的高斯中心，并用视觉语义特征初始化属性；2) 姿态感知特征学习：融合基础模型token、相机嵌入与标定射线信息，在投影3D位置聚合邻域视图特征；3) 高斯解码器：利用姿态感知特征细化高斯原语并渲染为语义占据场。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，VGOcc在纯视觉3D占据预测任务中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 引入基础模型的视觉-几何线索能有效增强高斯建模在占据预测中的表现，为纯视觉三维场景理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-only occupancy prediction requires recovering a semantic 3D occupancy field from calibrated surround-view images, where each view provides observations with ambiguous depth along camera rays. Existing methods have progressed from dense structured representations to sparse Gaussian primitives, improving the efficiency of 3D scene representation. However, Gaussian learning still relies primarily on image domain features, which provide limited explicit geometric information for volumetric reasoning. Our key observation is that effective Gaussian occupancy modeling requires not only sparse primitives, but also richer geometric and semantic learning cues. In this paper, we propose VGOcc, which learns visual and geometric cues from foundation models for Gaussian modeling. VGOcc incorporates these cues into primitive initialization and refinement, yielding a representation termed Visual-Geometric Gaussians tailored to semantic occupancy prediction. Specifically, we propose Visual-Geometric Gaussian Birth to form spatially balanced Gaussian centers from ray depth hypotheses, while visual semantic features initialize primitive attributes. Next, we design Pose-Aware Feature Learning to combine foundation tokens with camera embeddings and calibrated ray information. Features from neighboring views are then aggregated at projected 3D locations for each Gaussian refinement stage. Finally, Gaussian decoder refines birth Gaussians with pose-aware features and renders them into semantic occupancy. Experiments on nuScenes demonstrate that VGOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance in vision-only 3D occupancy prediction. Codes will be available at https://github.com/JHLin42in/VGOcc.

</details>


### [180] [GigaPath-Flash and GigaTIME-Flash: Efficient Pathology Foundation Models for Whole-Slide and Tumor Microenvironment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18218)
*Naoto Usuyama,Jeya Maria Jose Valanarasu,Sicong Yao,Hanwen Xu,Jaspreet Bagga,Guanghui Qin,Robert E. Kramer,Cliff Wong,Soohee Lee,Hao Qiu,Theodore Zhengde Zhao,Racheli Ben Shimol,Angela Crabtree,Kevin Matlock,Eduardo Alejandro Lozano Garcia,Naiteek Sangani,Alberto Santamaria-Pang,Jason Entenmann,Alexandra Q. Bartlett,Bill J. Wright,Bernard A. Fox,Brian Piening,Sheng Zhang,Sheng Wang,Tristan Naumann,Carlo Bifulco,Hoifung Poon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GigaPath-Flash和GigaTIME-Flash两个高效病理学基础模型。前者通过知识蒸馏将十亿参数模型压缩为4300万参数，在保持97%性能的同时降低50倍计算量；后者基于相同骨干预测肿瘤微环境，速度提升6倍、显存减少8倍。两个模型均采用Apache-2.0开源许可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型大多在图像块级别运行、计算成本高且许可限制严格，难以支撑大规模全切片级别的临床和研究应用。作者旨在开发高效、开放的全切片病理学AI模型。

Method: GigaPath-Flash采用22M参数的ViT-S切片编码器(从十亿参数GigaPath ViT-g教师模型蒸馏而来)结合21M参数的LongNet全切片编码器。GigaTIME-Flash基于相同骨干，预测H&E图像中的肿瘤免疫微环境。

Result: GigaPath-Flash保留GigaPath 97%的平均全切片性能，计算量减少50倍。GigaTIME-Flash预测质量超越原CNN版GigaTIME，速度提升6倍，GPU显存减少8倍。

Conclusion: 通过Apache-2.0开源发布完整模型族(GigaPath、GigaTIME、GigaPath-Flash、GigaTIME-Flash)，为计算病理学、免疫肿瘤学和精准健康提供了易获取的基础构建模块。

Abstract: Foundation models have emerged as a driving force in computational pathology, with the potential to transform cancer diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment selection by learning transferable representations from large-scale histopathology data. A growing landscape of pathology foundation models now spans diverse data sources, architectures, and downstream applications. However, most pretrained models operate only at the image-tile level, use restrictive licenses, and remain computationally expensive, limiting large-scale slide-level clinical and research use.
  Here, we introduce GigaPath-Flash and GigaTIME-Flash, efficient models for whole-slide pathology AI and spatial proteomics prediction. GigaPath-Flash combines a 22M-parameter ViT-S tile encoder with a 21M-parameter LongNet slide encoder, both pretrained on large-scale real-world histopathology data. Its compact tile encoder is distilled from the billion-parameter GigaPath (ViT-g) teacher and shared by both models. GigaPath-Flash retains 97% of GigaPath's average slide-level performance with 50x less compute. GigaTIME-Flash extends this backbone to predict the tumor immune microenvironment directly from routine H&E images. It surpasses the original CNN-based GigaTIME in prediction quality while running 6x faster and using 8x less GPU memory.
  Together with GigaPath and GigaTIME, these models form an open-weight, Apache-2.0-licensed family pretrained on large-scale real-world clinical data. By releasing all models and weights, we provide accessible building blocks for computational pathology, immuno-oncology, and precision health.

</details>


### [181] [Simple Domain Generalization for Strong Pixel-Level Image Tampering Detection in Modern VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18230)
*Yi Tang,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Cui,Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tran Dinh Tien,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Tianjun Yao,Yonina C. Eldar,Jing-Hao Xue,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个领域泛化框架用于检测现代VLM生成的像素级图像篡改，通过平衡小批量采样和后期注入策略，在OOD VLM上相比先前SOTA方法PIXAR取得了gIoU和cIoU分别提升26.1%和26.8%的相对改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLM（如ChatGPT、Gemini等）的图像生成与编辑能力日益增强，导致像素级图像篡改检测面临跨模型和分布外迁移的严峻挑战。现有方法在OOD场景下鲁棒性不足，亟需领域泛化的检测方案。

Method: 提出一个领域泛化训练框架，包含两个核心策略：(1) 平衡小批量采样——在每个minibatch中策略性地混合篡改图像与真实图像，防止优化偏向任一方向，避免训练崩溃；(2) 后期注入策略——先在大规模基础数据上训练检测器至稳定收敛，再引入少量来自新兴VLM分布的支撑数据微调，在不导致过拟合的前提下提升适应性。

Result: 在GPT-Images-2.0、Gemini-3.1、FLUX.2、Seedream 4.5等OOD VLM上，相比PIXAR方法，平均gIoU和cIoU分别相对提升26.1%和26.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架概念简单但效果显著，为现代VLM下的像素级篡改定位和OOD鲁棒性提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) have significantly improved image generation and editing capabilities, making pixel-level image tampering detection increasingly important yet challenging under cross-model and out-of-distribution shifts. This work studies domain generalization for pixel-level image tampering detection in modern VLMs like ChatGPT, Gemini, Qwen-Image, etc., aiming to learn tampering localization models that remain robust across diverse VLM-generated manipulation distributions. We propose a simple yet effective domain-generalized training framework built on two practical strategies. First, we introduce a balanced minibatch sampling scheme that strategically samples tampered and real images in each minibatch, preventing biased optimization toward either manipulated artifacts or clean-image priors and avoiding training collapse, ensuring that each optimization step receives proper sampled gradient signals. Second, we adopt a simple late-injection strategy, where the detector is first trained on large-scale base data until stable convergence, and then exposed to a small amount of newly selected supporting data from emerging VLM distributions, improving adaptability without overfitting to limited new domains. Together, these components provide a simple yet strong recipe for improving pixel-level tampering localization and OOD robustness across modern VLMs. Despite the conceptual simplicity, our framework outperforms the prior state-of-the-art PIXAR by a large margin of 26.1% and 26.8% relative improvement in average gIoU and cIoU, respectively, across OOD VLMs of GPT-Images-2.0, Gemini-3.1, FLUX.2, and Seedream 4.5. Our code is available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/PIXAR-DG

</details>


### [182] [SciForma: Structure-Faithful Generation of Scientific Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18091)
*Yuxuan Luo,Peng Zhang,Xinjie Zhang,Xun Guo,Zhouhui Lian,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciForma框架，通过结构清单分解、多维度合取偏好优化（M-DPO）和推理时迭代编辑，解决了科学方法论图表生成中的结构保真度问题，9B模型超越了所有开源基线和GPT-Image-1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法论图表的结构保真度至关重要，但当前开源模型无法满足这一要求：SFT只能学习合理布局但无法保证结构正确性，标量奖励的后训练则无法定位具体哪个结构维度失败。结构保真度具有"合取性"（一个轴的正确不能补偿另一轴的失败），现有方法缺乏对这种多维度联合正确性的建模。

Method: 1) 将图表质量分解为Component、Arrow、Text三个结构轴，构建结构清单（structural inventory）；2) 基于此清单构建SciFormaData-700K训练数据和SciFormaBench-2K评估基准；3) 提出多维度合取偏好优化（M-DPO），在三个轴上同时施加正确性约束，并自适应地将梯度路由到最薄弱的维度；4) 利用结构清单在推理时进行迭代编辑以修正残余错误。

Result: SciForma-9B在SciFormaBench-2K和AIBench上超越了所有开源基线模型以及GPT-Image-1.5，将开源科学图表生成的结构保真度提升至接近闭源模型水平。

Conclusion: 结构维度的分解+多维度合取优化+推理时编辑的组合是解决复杂结构化图表生成的有效路径，证明了在结构化生成任务中，显式建模"合取性"约束比单一的标量奖励更优。

Abstract: Structural fidelity is essential to scientific methodology diagrams. To communicate research logic, these diagrams must faithfully render components, directional relations, and textual annotations. Since a single error, such as a reversed arrow or an unreadable equation, can invalidate the entire figure, structural fidelity is inherently conjunctive: correctness on one axis cannot compensate for failure on another. Current open-source models fail to satisfy this criterion. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) learns plausible layouts but cannot reliably ensure structural correctness, while scalar reward-based post-training obscures which structural dimension has failed. To address this, we introduce SciForma, a framework for the structure faithful generation of scientific methodology diagrams. Specifically, SciForma decomposes diagram quality into three structural axes: Component, Arrow, and Text, guided by a structural inventory. Built on this foundation, we curate SciFormaData-700K for structured training and SciFormaBench-2K for logic-verified evaluation. To close the gap left by SFT, we develop Multi-Dimensional Conjunctive Preference Optimization (M-DPO), which enforces simultaneous correctness across all axes and adaptively routes gradients to the most deficient dimension in post-training. The same structural inventory also enables iterative editing at inference time to correct residual errors. This combination allows SciForma-9B to exceed all open-source baselines and GPT-Image-1.5 on both SciFormaBench-2K and AIBench, bringing open scientific diagram generation close to proprietary-level structural fidelity. Our code and data will be available at: https://github.com/microsoft/SciForma.

</details>


### [183] [SpEmoC: A Balanced Speaker-Segment Multimodal Emotion Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18109)
*Sania Bano,Shahzad Ahmad,Santosh Kumar Vipparthi,Sukalpa Chanda,Subrahmanyam Murala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpEmoC，一个包含30,000个高质量视频片段的多模态情感数据集，涵盖7种情绪类别，通过严格的电影/剧集级划分和类别平衡设计，验证了平衡数据和合理数据划分对跨数据集体泛化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感数据集在规模、情绪分布、模态对齐和数据划分策略上不一致，导致跨数据集泛化和少数类情绪建模不可靠，缺乏一个设计规范、类别平衡的多模态情感数据集。

Method: 从3100部英语电影和电视剧中提取306,544个原始片段，筛选出30,000个高质量、类别平衡的片段。采用预训练模型结合人工验证的混合标注流程，对齐视觉、音频和文本三种模态，并采用严格的电影/剧集级划分防止数据泄漏。

Result: 领域内基准测试、跨数据集迁移、低数据训练、类别不平衡分析和模态迁移实验均表明，平衡的数据分布和严格的数据划分使得模型在跨数据集体评估时情绪表现更稳定。

Conclusion: 数据集设计（类别平衡与严格划分）对构建鲁棒且可迁移的多模态情绪识别模型至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding human emotions in spoken conversations is a key challenge in affective computing, with applications in empathetic AI, human computer interaction, and mental health monitoring. However, existing datasets vary in scale, emotion distribution, modality alignment, and data partitioning strategies, which can influence reliable cross-dataset generalization and minority-emotion modeling. We introduce SpEmoC a Speaking segment Emotion for Conversations comprising 306,544 raw clips from 3,100 English language movies and TV series. From these, 30,000 high quality, class balanced clips are curated, featuring synchronized visual, audio, and textual modalities annotated for seven emotions through a hybrid pipeline that integrates pretrained models with human validation. SpEmoC uses strict movie- and series-level splits to prevent content overlap between split sets, allowing more reliable evaluation of model generalization. The dataset also maintains a near-balanced distribution across seven emotions, including minority classes such as Fear and Disgust, which supports more balanced learning across categories. Extensive experiments, including in-domain benchmarking, cross-dataset transfer, low-data training, class-imbalance analysis, and modality transfer show that balanced data and careful splitting lead to more stable performance across emotions when models are evaluated on other datasets. These results highlight the importance of dataset design for robust and transferable multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [184] [Occlusion-Aware Panoptic Segmentation with Joint Position Embedding and Occlusion-Level Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18112)
*Wenbo Wei,Jun Wang,Shan Raza,Abhir Bhalerao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PEMOLA模块，利用遮挡级注意力与位置嵌入调制，在transformer-based全景分割中引入遮挡先验，显著提升遮挡场景下的分割质量，并构建了Cityscapes-OLAC数据集以验证跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂场景中的全景分割常因遮挡导致性能下降，但现有方法普遍忽视显式的遮挡建模，缺乏高效的遮挡感知机制。

Method: 1) 在COCO-OLAC上训练遮挡分类器，输出遮挡级注意力（空间指导）与遮挡标签的嵌入（通道权重）；2) 通过联合调制将遮挡先验融入位置嵌入；3) 构建Cityscapes-OLAC数据集（沿用COCO-OLAC标注协议）用于跨数据集评估。

Result: 在COCO-OLAC和Cityscapes-OLAC上，PEMOLA一致提升了全景分割质量，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 遮挡建模对鲁棒全景分割至关重要，PEMOLA通过融入遮挡级注意力有效提升了遮挡场景下的分割性能。

Abstract: Panoptic segmentation in complex scenes remains challenging because of occlusions, yet modern approaches often neglect occlusion modelling. In this paper, we propose \textbf{P}osition \textbf{E}mbedding \textbf{M}odulation with \textbf{O}cclusion-\textbf{L}evel \textbf{A}ttention (PEMOLA), a novel occlusion-aware module that can be seamlessly integrated into transformer-based panoptic segmentation. To obtain occlusion cues, we train an occlusion classifier on the COCO-OLAC dataset. The classifier derives the occlusion-level attention, which serves as spatial guidance, while the occlusion labels are encoded into a learnable embedding to produce channel-wise weights. Through joint modulation, PEMOLA elegantly introduces the occlusion priors into the position embedding, thereby improving the occlusion modelling. We further annotate the Cityscapes dataset with occlusion levels, termed Cityscapes Occlusion Labels for All Computer Vision Tasks (Cityscapes-OLAC), following the same labelling protocol as COCO-OLAC, to evaluate the cross-dataset generalisation ability of PEMOLA. Extensive experiments on COCO-OLAC and Cityscapes-OLAC demonstrate that PEMOLA consistently improves panoptic segmentation quality while introducing minimal computational overhead. These results highlight the importance of occlusion modelling, where incorporating occlusion-level attention helps deliver robust panoptic segmentation under occlusion. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/wenbo-wei/PEMOLA.

</details>


### [185] [Lossless-INR: Lossless Volumetric Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18150)
*Kaiyuan Tang,Daniel Burke,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lossless-INR框架，基于位平面分解、八叉树块划分和三元特征网格网络，首次实现了三维科学体数据的无损隐式神经表示，达到零比特误差和位精确重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示（INR）的体数据方法本质上有损，即使微小的重建误差也会通过渲染和下游分析传播，影响结果可靠性。因此需要一种无损的INR框架来保证数据精确恢复。

Method: 1) 位平面分解：将每个体素值分解为二进制位平面，将重建任务转化为逐比特二分类问题；2) 八叉树块划分：自适应细分复杂区域，保持表示紧凑；3) 三元特征网格网络：网格条目由三元值集合参数化，使优化可处理。

Result: 在多种体数据集上实现零比特错误率和位精确重建，支持保真渲染和下游分析，同时保持紧凑表示。

Conclusion: Lossless-INR成功证明了隐式神经表示可以实现无损体数据压缩与重建，为科学可视化等需要高保真度的应用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) methods provide continuous coordinate-to-value mappings and integrate naturally with direct volume rendering, making them attractive for representing volumetric data. However, existing INR-based approaches for volumetric data are inherently lossy, and even small reconstruction errors can propagate through rendering and downstream analysis. In this work, we explore Lossless-INR, a lossless INR framework for 3D scientific volumetric data based on bit-plane decomposition. By decomposing each voxel value into binary bit-planes, we reformulate reconstruction as per-bit binary classification, so that exact recovery reduces to predicting every bit correctly. To make this optimization tractable while keeping the representation compact, we combine an octree block-partitioning strategy that adaptively subdivides complex regions with a ternary feature-grid network whose grid entries are parameterized by a ternary set of values. Experiments on diverse volumetric datasets show that this design can achieve zero bit-error rate and bit-exact reconstruction, enabling faithful rendering and downstream analysis with a compact representation. The code is available at https://github.com/TouKaienn/Lossless-INR.

</details>


### [186] [Plenoptic Condensation: A Novel Approach to Generalized Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18151)
*Brevin Tilmon,Alex DeJournett,John Leffingwell,Scott Ackerson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Plenoptic Condensation (PCon) 方法，通过多阶段重建流水线将图像先转化为低功率的“汤状”场景元素，再自适应浓缩为高功率的“结构化”元素。该方法生成 Reality Models (Relms)，支持空间可变表示能力，在消费级手机和无人机拍摄的场景中实现了远超 SOTA 方法（NeRO、RT-Splatting）的重建精度，尤其在局部损伤测量上达到 35μm 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义场景重建方法在表示能力上存在局限，难以同时高效处理尖锐边缘、光滑反射面等不同特征，且缺乏空间可变的表示能力，导致高保真渲染和精确测量的需求难以满足。

Method: 提出 PCon 多阶段重建流水线：(1) 将图像转化为低功率的“汤状”场景元素；(2) 自适应地将“汤”浓缩为高功率的“结构化”元素，以高效表示尖锐边缘和光滑反射面。最终生成 Reality Models (Relms)，实现空间可变的表示能力。

Result: 在 "Damaged Fiat" 案例中，PCon 对引擎盖的重建精度是 SOTA 方法（NeRO、RT-Splatting）的两倍以上；局部损伤剖面误差达 35μm，而 SOTA 方法基本无法测量损伤。

Conclusion: PCon 通过自适应浓缩策略实现了空间可变的表示能力，在消费级设备采集的数据上取得了远超现有方法的精度，尤其在微观损伤测量方面展现出独特优势，为广义场景重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a novel Generalized Scene Reconstruction (GSR) approach called Plenoptic Condensation (PCon). PCon uses a multi-stage reconstruction pipeline, initially converting images into "soupy" scene elements with low (representational) power, then adaptively condensing the "soup" into "structured" elements of higher power capable of efficiently representing, for example, sharp edges and smooth reflective surfaces. PCon scene models called Reality Models (Relms) enable spatially varying representational power, which is essential for high-fidelity rendering, measurement, and scene understanding. We showcase several in-the-wild PCon reconstructions captured with consumer phone cameras and drones. In one case called "Damaged Fiat", PCon is benchmarked against two state-of-the-art (SOTA) GSR methods: NeRO and RT-Splatting. Referring to Figure 1 below, PCon reconstructs the car hood more than twice as accurately as the SOTA methods. But more importantly, the local damage profile error for PCon is 35 um (0.035 mm), whereas the two other SOTA methods are essentially unable to measure the damage at all. Our project website is available at https://quidient.github.io/pcon-2026.html.

</details>


### [187] [Robust Multimodal Dynamic Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18153)
*Zhe Xin,Hanzhi Chang,Penghui Huang,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个融合2D点轨迹、3D重建和语义信息的多模态动态物体分割框架，结合Transformer与特征聚类聚合模块进行分类，并引入基于点查询的SAM后处理方法，在动态分割和静态场景重建任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流法在物体边界处无法保证一致的分割结果，而3D重建方法对重建误差高度敏感，因此需要一种能同时保证分割精确性和完整性的动态物体分割方法。

Method: 1) 融合2D点轨迹、3D重建和语义信息的多模态特征；2) 结合Transformer和特征聚类聚合模块进行静态/动态分类；3) 自适应决定不同场景中哪种模态特征占主导；4) 引入基于点查询的SAM后处理方法处理单掩码中的多物体问题。

Result: 在动态物体分割和静态场景重建任务上均达到最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 多模态融合方法能有效克服单一模态的局限性，自适应机制和SAM后处理进一步提升了分割的精确性和完整性。

Abstract: Dynamic object segmentation plays a critical role in many visual applications such as static scene reconstruction from dynamic videos. However, existing optical flow-based methods fail to ensure consistent static/dynamic segmentation along object boundaries, while 3D reconstruction-based approaches are highly sensitive to reconstruction errors. To address these limitations, we present a dynamic object segmentation framework that can generate both precise and complete dynamic masks by integrating multimodal cues including 2D point tracks, 3D reconstruction, and semantic information. We design a network combining Transformer architectures with feature clustering aggregation modules to perform static/dynamic classification of multimodal feature trajectories. It enables the model to adaptively determine which type of feature should dominate based on the characteristics of each scene, while also mitigating the impact of feature degradation. Additionally, we introduce a novel point-query-based SAM post-processing method capable of handling multiple objects within a single mask. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in both dynamic object segmentation and static scene reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Certified Training for Convolutional Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18195)
*Benedikt Brückner,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的认证训练方法，通过对卷积扰动进行高效编码，训练出对运动模糊等扰动具有可证明鲁棒性的视觉模型。在CIFAR10上，该方法针对合理强度的运动模糊取得了超过80%的鲁棒准确率，同时保持了可比的常规准确率，显著优于对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型对运行时运动模糊等扰动高度敏感，可能导致关键应用（如目标检测）失败。现有的数据增强和对抗训练等方法虽然能提升经验鲁棒性，但缺乏形式化的安全保障，难以识别和缓解隐藏的脆弱性。

Method: 提出一种认证训练方法，通过高效编码卷积扰动来训练具有可证明鲁棒性的模型。该方法将运动模糊等扰动形式化建模为卷积操作，并在训练过程中利用认证机制保证模型对一定范围内的扰动具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在CIFAR10数据集上，针对合理强度的运动模糊，该方法取得了超过80%的鲁棒准确率，同时保持了可比的常规准确率，显著优于对抗训练方法。

Conclusion: 该认证训练方法能够为视觉模型提供形式化的安全保障，有效抵御运动模糊等卷积扰动，且不影响模型在正常情况下的表现，为模型在关键应用中的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision models have been found to be susceptible to perturbations such as motion blur induced at runtime by a shaking camera. This impedes their deployment in critical applications since phenomena such as slightly blurred vision might lead to failures, for example an object detector missing objects. While methods such as data augmentation or Adversarial Training can improve empirical robustness, they lack formal safety guarantees, making it difficult to identify and mitigate hidden vulnerabilities. We introduce a novel Certified Training approach that leverages an efficient encoding of convolutional perturbations to train provably robust models. Our method significantly outperforms Adversarial Training, achieving, for example, over 80% robust accuracy against motion blur of reasonable intensity on CIFAR10 while maintaining comparable standard accuracy.

</details>


### [189] [HOMIE: Human-object Centric Video Personalization via Multimodal Intelligent Enchancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18217)
*Yiyang Cai,Nan Chen,Rongchang Xie,Junwen Pan,Chunyang Jiang,Cheng Chen,Wen Zhou,Zhenbang Sun,Wei Xue,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOMIE是一个统一处理主体间和主体内参考设置的人-物交互视频个性化生成框架，通过改进MLLM集成策略、全局多模态引导和模态参考嵌入，在保持文本可控性的同时提升了主体保真度和交互准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在保持高主体保真度与准确的人-物交互模式之间存在权衡，尤其当物体为抽象概念（如logo）时表现不佳；同时缺乏理解主体内参考（如OCR地图、多视角输入）中潜在对应关系的机制。

Method: 提出HOMIE框架：(1) 改进MLLM集成策略，在不牺牲文本编码器可控性且避免昂贵的重新对齐的前提下提取参考级关系知识；(2) 在自注意力中引入全局多模态引导，更好地对齐MLLM提取的语义特征与VAE令牌；(3) 提出模态参考嵌入，区分MLLM特征与VAE令牌，并关联主体内参考图像令牌。

Result: 在各种HOCVP任务中达到了最先进的性能（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: HOMIE以统一框架有效解决了主体间和主体内两种参考设置下的挑战，为HOCVP任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-object centric video personalization (HOCVP) is a core task within subject-driven video generation. However, existing methods suffer from two key limitations. First, most approaches focusing on inter-subject personalization still struggle to strike a balance between high subject fidelity and accurate interaction patterns between humans and diverse objects, especially when objects represent abstract concepts such as logos. Second, while intra-subject references (e.g., OCR maps, multi-view inputs) are expected to enhance subject fidelity, most existing works lack mechanisms to understand such latent correspondence. To address both challenges, we propose HOMIE, an HOCVP framework that tackles both inter- and intra-subject input settings in a unified manner. Compared to previous approaches, HOMIE proposes a better MLLM integration strategy to extract knowledge of reference-level relationships without compromising the controllability of text encoders or incurring costly re-alignment. Specifically, we introduce global multimodal guidance within self-attention to better align MLLM-derived semantic features with VAE tokens. Furthermore, we propose modality-reference embedding to differentiate tokens from MLLM features and VAE tokens and associate intra-subject reference image tokens. Extensive experiments validate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across various HOCVP tasks. Project Page: https://yiyangcai.github.io/homie-page.github.io/

</details>


### [190] [FlowMimic: Mask-free Visual Editing and Generation with Pixel-pair Warped Flow Field for Online Video Editing Data Generation and Modality Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18227)
*Dingyun Zhang,Lixue Gong,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的视频与图像生成编辑模型，通过像素对时间扭曲流场实时从图像编辑样本生成视频编辑样本，并设计模态模仿损失对齐两种模态能力，同时引入感知相关任务和编辑区域感知损失使模型内化区域定位能力，无需外部辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑数据收集方法依赖人工标注、I2V模型合成和VLM过滤，过程耗时且易引入错误，导致视频编辑任务多样性远不如图像编辑。同时，现有语言驱动的视觉编辑方法过度依赖外部辅助（如微调额外的MLLM或显式提供掩码序列），希望模型能内化这些能力。

Method: 1) 像素对时间扭曲流场：直接从图像编辑样本实时生成对应的视频编辑样本。2) 模态模仿生成损失与模态模仿编辑损失：通过相互模仿对齐图像和视频模态的能力与输出分布。3) 感知相关任务（如指代表达分割）及编辑区域感知的隐空间损失和注意力损失，使模型内化编辑区域定位能力。

Result: 在多个层级的视频编辑任务上证明，模型仅使用该方法生成的数据即可学习视频编辑，并且实现了图像与视频模态能力的统一与对齐。

Conclusion: 本文探索了统一的图像-视频生成编辑框架，通过高效的数据生成方式、模态对齐损失以及内化的区域定位能力，有效克服了现有方法的局限，拓展了视频编辑的任务多样性。

Abstract: In line with the prevailing direction of vision research, we explore the integration of both generation and editing capabilities for video and image modalities within a single model. Current approaches to collecting video editing data typically depend on labour-intensive, time-consuming curated procedures--involving object mask annotation, the use of error-introducing pair synthesis via I2V model and ControlNet-like guidance, and VLM-based quality filtering or refinement--and demonstrate limited task scalability. As a result, the diversity of editing tasks remains substantially narrower than that available for image editing models. We develop a pixel-pair temporal warped flow field that can directly generate corresponding video editing samples in real time from image editing samples, and we demonstrate across multiple levels of video editing tasks that a model can learn video editing using only such data. We regard the image modality as a particular form of the video modality. Accordingly, we design a modality mimic generation loss and a modality mimic editing loss to relatively align the capabilities--and thereby the output distributions--of the two modalities through mutual imitation. Moreover, language-based visual editing entails the comprehension of the editing instruction and the reference visual content, the localization of the region corresponding to that instruction within the reference visual contents, and the modification of that region alone. Existing approaches predominantly rely on external aids, such as fine-tuning an additional MLLM or explicitly supplying a mask sequence as auxiliary input during inference. In contrast, we aspire for the model to internalize this capability. To that end, we introduce sense-related tasks--for instance, referring expression segmentation--along with corresponding editing-region-aware latent-level loss and attention-level loss.

</details>


### [191] [The Many Senses of Visual Similarity: A Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18237)
*Sheng-Yu Wang,Yotam Nitzan,Aaron Hertzmann,Jun-Yan Zhu,Eli Shechtman,Alexei A. Efros,Richard Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TPIPS，一种基于文本提示的图像感知相似度度量，通过大规模三元组人类相似性判断数据集微调视觉语言模型，使其能够根据文本条件捕捉不同语义层面的视觉相似性，在多个任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知相似度度量将不同维度（如形状、颜色）的相似性坍缩为单一标量值，无法根据特定方面进行条件化判断，与人类上下文依赖的视觉相似性判断方式存在差距。

Method: 构建大规模图像三元组人类相似性判断数据集，每个三元组在多个自由形式的语义方面进行标注；在此基础上微调视觉语言模型（VLM），使其能够根据文本提示输出条件化的感知相似度分数（TPIPS）。

Result: TPIPS比现有前沿视觉语言模型更接近人类标注者共识；在训练分布外也能可靠泛化；在文本引导检索、组合搜索和生成模型细粒度评估中展现出新能力。

Conclusion: TPIPS通过文本提示实现了多维度、可条件化的视觉相似性度量，弥合了机器与人类在相似性判断上的差距，并为多项下游任务提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Human visual similarity judgments are context-dependent. For example, two images may be similar in shape but distinct in color. Existing perceptual similarity metrics, however, collapse these nuances into a single scalar value, offering no mechanism to condition on specific aspects. To bridge this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset of human similarity judgments over image triplets, where each triplet is annotated across multiple, free-form semantic aspects of similarity. Benchmarking a broad range of frontier vision-language models (VLMs) reveals a considerable performance gap compared to human annotators' consensus. Leveraging our data, we fine-tune a VLM to produce our Text-Prompted Image Perceptual Similarity (TPIPS) metric, capturing multiple senses of visual similarity depending on the specified text prompt. We demonstrate that TPIPS aligns more closely with human perception and generalizes reliably beyond the training distribution. Finally, we show that TPIPS unlocks new capabilities in text-guided retrieval, compositional search, and the fine-grained evaluation of generative models. Our code, data, and trained models are at https://peterwang512.github.io/TPIPS

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [HyperDCM: Dynamic Cluster Memory Replay in Hyperbolic Space for Continual Robotic Navigation Across Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16267)
*Zhengfei Lu,Jian Yang,Muyu Wang,Shaowen Chen,Jinpeng Mi,Ke Li,Xiong You,Qi Wu,Xuan Tang,Xian Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视觉导航中的灾难性遗忘和适应性难题，提出基于双曲动态聚类记忆（HyperDCM）的扩散策略导航方法，通过场景图建模、双曲空间投影和结构感知记忆回放，在室内外多场景数据上超越现有持续学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中视觉导航面临灾难性遗忘和难以适应多样变化环境的问题，现有方法无法有效保留结构化知识。

Method: 1) 用大视觉语言模型从RGB观测中提取语义场景三元组；2) 通过R-GCN编码为场景图嵌入；3) 投影到双曲空间增强结构可分性和记忆保持；4) 采用动态聚类和结构敏感更新策略选择代表性样本进行记忆回放。

Result: 在室内外多场景数据集上，HyperDCM相比多种持续学习基线方法，在保留过去导航能力和泛化性能上均更优。

Conclusion: 结构感知的记忆机制能有效缓解灾难性遗忘，双曲空间为持续视觉导航提供了更好的知识保留结构。

Abstract: Continual learning in visual navigation remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting and the difficulties associated with adapting to diverse and evolving environments. To address these issues, we propose Hyperbolic Dynamic Cluster Memory (HyperDCM), a structure-aware memory mechanism that enhances diffusion policy-based navigation through scene graph modeling and principled memory replay. HyperDCM extracts semantic scene triples from RGB observations using large vision-language models, encodes them into scene graph embeddings via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (R-GCN), and projects the embeddings into hyperbolic space to enhance structural separability and retention in continual navigation. A dynamic clustering and structure-sensitive update strategy selects representative samples for memory replay, thereby preserving knowledge diversity and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Experiments on multi-scene indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that HyperDCM achieves superior retention of past navigation capabilities and improved generalization compared to representative continual learning baselines adapted to diffusion policy navigation.

</details>


### [193] [Control Design for a Rideable Animatronic Two-Wheeled Robot with Quadruped Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16302)
*Tadashi Sumioka,Kazushi Akimoto,Takuya Tsujimura,Sho Takayanagi,Yuya Horiuchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种兼具两轮自平衡底座和四肢仿生运动的人形载具机器人，通过将主要 locomotion 交给轮式底座、四肢用于辅助和表现，实现了安全、自然且富有表现力的动态骑行体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来摩托车在年轻群体中受欢迎度下降，作者希望打造一款拥有四肢的未来伙伴型两轮载具机器人，用于公共活动，重新激发年轻人对摩托车的兴趣。

Method: 机器人以自平衡两轮底座作为主要移动方式，四肢在上下车时提供辅助支撑，并在移动时与轮速协调运动，产生四足步态效果；同时提出了鲁棒的自平衡控制方法（抵抗四肢快速运动的扰动）以及产生自然四足行为的运动控制策略。

Result: 实现了能够在快速四肢运动下保持稳定的自平衡控制，以及能够产生类四足自然步态的运动控制策略。

Conclusion: 该机器人通过轮式+四肢协同的设计，在保证安全、匹配角色规格的前提下，实现了直观的重心偏移控制与自由自然的动态表达，为未来伙伴型移动机器人提供了新思路。

Abstract: In recent years, motorcycle popularity has been declining, particularly among the younger generations. To rekindle interest in motorcycles among this demographic, we developed a rideable two-wheeled robot equipped with four limbs as a future partner mobility concept and intended for use in public events. The character reproduced by this robot carries the protagonist on its back and exhibits a dynamic quadrupedal gait. To maximize the riding experience, we aimed to match the robot's weight and size to the character's specifications while ensuring rider safety and enabling expressive movements of limbs, wrists, ankles, and facial features. However, achieving locomotion solely through limb movement would require excessive motor output and increased limb strength, resulting in higher weight and extremely slow gait, thereby reducing character fidelity. To overcome these challenges, the robot performs its primary locomotion using a self-balancing two-wheeled base, while the limbs provide auxiliary support during mounting and dismounting and move in coordination with the wheeled locomotion speed. This approach enables an animatronic robot capable of intuitive, weight-shift-based control for free and natural movement. In this paper, we focus on a robust selfbalancing control method that maintains stability even during rapid limb movements, as well as a motion control strategy that generates natural quadruped-like behavior.

</details>


### [194] [Real-Time sEMG-Based Telecontrol of an Assistive Robotic Arm Using a 1D Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16310)
*Edgar Manacorda,Mena Samir Kama Abouseffien,Olivier Lecompte,Amandine Gesta,Abolfazl Mohebbi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表面肌电信号(sEMG)的实时接口，用于辅助机器人手臂的遥操作，采用四通道sEMG采集、一维卷积神经网络(CNN)分类，实现了90%以上的分类准确率和约0.32秒的平均延迟，在仿真和真实机器人平台上均验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢运动障碍严重影响日常生活中的物体操作能力，辅助机器人手臂虽能提供帮助，但需要直观、可靠且响应迅速的控制接口。sEMG作为一种非侵入式获取肌肉活动及用户运动意图的手段，有望解决这一控制问题。

Method: 采用四通道sEMG信号采集、预处理、滑动窗口分割，以及一维CNN分类器。探索了三种实时策略：基于阈值的动作起始检测、两级分类（静息vs运动+手势识别）、单分类器同时处理静息和五种手势。完整系统在仿真和真实机器人平台上进行了评估。

Result: CNN方法分类准确率超过90%，对实验采集信号具有良好的泛化能力；系统实时稳定，平均延迟约0.32秒；机器人在仿真和真实环境中均能通过离散手势产生连贯平滑的运动。

Conclusion: 证明了基于sEMG的遥控制辅助机器人手臂的可行性，强调了信号处理、深度学习与控制策略在统一实时框架中集成的重要性。未来可探索结合额外传感模态的混合控制方法以进一步提升鲁棒性和可用性。

Abstract: Motor impairments affecting the upper limb significantly reduce autonomy in daily activities, particularly for tasks involving object manipulation. Assistive robotic arms offer a promising solution, provided they can be controlled in an intuitive, reliable, and responsive manner. Among human--machine interface approaches, surface electromyography (sEMG) enables non-invasive access to muscle activity and thus to the user's motor intentions. This work proposes a real-time sEMG-based interface for the teleoperation of an assistive robotic arm. The system relies on four-channel sEMG acquisition, signal preprocessing, segmentation into sliding windows, and classification using a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN). Several real-time strategies are investigated, including threshold-based onset detection, a two-stage classification approach (rest vs movement followed by gesture recognition), and a single classifier handling both rest and five gestures. The complete pipeline is implemented and evaluated both in simulation and on a real robotic platform. The CNN-based approach achieves high classification performance, with a test accuracy above 90\% and strong generalization on experimentally acquired signals. The system exhibits stable real-time behavior, with an average latency of approximately 0.32 s consistent with the chosen windowing strategy, and the robot can be controlled reliably using discrete gestures, producing coherent and smooth movements in both simulated and real environments. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of sEMG-based telecontrol for assistive robotics and highlight the importance of integrating signal processing, deep learning, and control strategies within a unified real-time framework. Future work may explore hybrid control approaches combining sEMG with additional sensing modalities to further improve robustness and usability.

</details>


### [195] [S.E.A.G.R: A Socially and Emotionally Aware Greeting Robot Framework with Dual-Layer Cultural and Affective Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16341)
*Sajjad Hussain,Ranveer Bhura,Amandip Dutta,Shwetangshu Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAGR（社交与情感感知问候机器人）框架，通过文化身份和情感状态双层调制机制，实现跨文化背景和不同情绪状态下的人机交互问候行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人问候系统多采用静态程序，无法适应文化差异、情感语境和人际距离变化，影响用户的第一印象、舒适度和信任感。

Method: 采用Sense-Think-Act统一架构，结合文化映射、基于情感的手势调制和人际距离调节，实现双层调制框架：文化身份决定问候类型，情感线索影响问候执行方式。

Result: 实现低成本原型系统（USB摄像头、超声波传感器、Arduino控制的舵机和笔记本电脑Python系统），但当前仅为系统设计与概念验证，缺乏实证用户研究验证。

Conclusion: SEAGR为社交机器人的文化自适应问候提供了系统化框架，未来需通过用户研究进行实证验证，以检验其在真实多文化场景中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents SEAGR (Socially and Emotionally Aware Greeting Robot), a robotic greeting framework designed for human-robot interaction environments involving users from diverse cultural backgrounds and different emotional states. Since greeting behaviour strongly influences first impressions, user comfort, and trust, robots operating in public spaces must be able to interact in a socially appropriate and adaptive manner. However, many existing systems still rely on static greeting routines that do not account for cultural variation, emotional context, or interpersonal distance. SEAGR introduces a dual-layer modulation framework in which cultural identity determines the appropriate greeting type, while affective cues influence how that greeting is executed. The system combines context-aware cultural mapping, emotion-based gesture modulation, and proxemic regulation within a unified Sense-Think-Act architecture. A low-cost prototype is implemented using a USB camera, ultrasonic sensor, Arduino-controlled servos, and a laptop-based Python processing system. This work is presented as a system design and proof-of-concept; empirical validation through user studies is explicitly acknowledged as a current limitation and is identified as the primary direction for future work.

</details>


### [196] [PRISM: Multimodal Terrain Mapping for Rover Navigation in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16366)
*Raul Castilla-Arquillo,Carlos Perez-del-Pulgar,Levin Gerdes,Alfonso Garcia-Cerezo,Miguel A. Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM多模态感知系统，通过RGB-D-T传感器融合和OmniUnet视觉Transformer网络实现非结构化环境中的地形分割与可通行性制图，在资源受限嵌入式平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中机器人导航需要鲁棒的地形感知能力，传统单模态传感器难以准确区分陡坡、岩石等危险地形，亟需多模态融合方法提升地形测绘的可靠性。

Method: 搭建RGB-D-T多模态传感器套件；提出OmniUnet视觉Transformer网络进行语义地形分割；构建BASEPROD和LAENTIEC两个标注数据集；在嵌入式计算机上部署并生成可通行性地图。

Result: 新标注数据集上的验证结果和实地物理实验均证明了PRISM系统的有效性，能够为GNC子系统提供可通行性地图支持自主导航。

Conclusion: PRISM通过多模态融合（尤其热成像）显著提升了非结构化环境下的地形感知能力，且具备在资源受限平台上实时运行的可行性。

Abstract: Robotic navigation in unstructured environments requires robust situational awareness to safely traverse hazards such as steep slopes and rocky terrain. To address this challenge, perception systems increasingly rely on multimodal sensor fusion. Specifically, integrating thermal imagery with standard optical and depth sensors enhances terrain differentiation, directly improving the reliability of mapping algorithms. This paper presents PRISM, a multimodal perception system for terrain mapping in unstructured settings. PRISM leverages a custom sensor suite to capture aligned RGB, depth, and thermal (RGB-D-T) imagery. At its core is OmniUnet, a novel vision transformer-based network specifically designed for multimodal semantic terrain segmentation. We validated the proposed system using two newly annotated datasets (BASEPROD and LAENTIEC) and demonstrate its real-world applicability through physical field experiments. Deployed on a resource-constrained embedded computer, PRISM efficiently generates traversability maps that directly enable autonomous navigation via a rover's Guidance, Navigation, and Control (GNC) subsystem.

</details>


### [197] [Back to the museum: Investigation of the acceptance of Android Andrea with and without emotion simulation in a museum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16428)
*Marcel Heisler,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在德国博物馆中，仿人机器人Andrea自主与游客进行多语言对话，通过三种情感模拟条件（无情感、ChatGPT 4.1驱动情感、WASABI架构模拟情感）进行实验，73名游客的问卷反馈显示前两种情感实现方法对主观评价无显著积极影响，且未在意识层面被察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在自主对话仿人机器人中实现情感模拟是否能够提升用户体验，以及不同情感模拟方法（大语言模型驱动 vs. 专用情感架构）的效果差异。

Method: 在德国博物馆部署仿人机器人Andrea，进行为期六天的全自主多语言对话；设置三种实验条件（无情感、ChatGPT 4.1驱动情感、WASABI架构模拟情感）；采用扩展版TAM2问卷收集73名游客的主观评价。

Result: ChatGPT 4.1驱动的情感和无情感条件均未对游客的主观评价产生显著积极影响，且情感的存在未被游客在意识层面察觉。

Conclusion: 当前的情感模拟方法（尤其是基于LLM的方式）在仿人机器人对话中未能带来可量化的用户体验提升，暗示更加精细或隐性的情感表达机制可能更为关键。

Abstract: For a second time, the android robot Andrea was set up at a public museum in Germany for six consecutive days to have conversations with visitors, fully autonomously. Building on previously gathered qualitative results, the robot was now capable of engaging in multi-lingual conversation with the visitors about the museum context. The robot was prepared with context information about the museum in general and its surrounding exhibits this time. The robot featured a slightly artificial sounding voice that was previously evaluated as congruent with its gender-ambiguous but very humanlike design. Three experimental conditions were implemented, in which either (1) the robot simulated no emotions, (2) the robots emotions were determined by ChatGPT 4.1, or (3) the WASABI emotion simulation architecture simulated the robot's emotion dynamics. An extended version of the TAM2 questionnaire was employed to let 73 visitors report on several factors of their opinion about the android robot Andrea after having experienced it. In result, the statistical analysis suggests that these first two approaches to implementing emotions into the chat architecture of our android robot Andrea did not yield any positive effects on the subjective evaluations by the visitors and were not detectable on a conscious level.

</details>


### [198] [Linear Stability Analysis of an INDI Pitch-Rate Controller under Model Mismatch for a Tilt-Rotor VTOL UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16471)
*Lorenzo Schenk,Guillaume Ducard,Christopher Onder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了倾转旋翼垂直起降无人机中增量非线性动态反演（INDI）控制器的线性稳定性，推导了俯仰速率/升降副翼子通道的五阶传递函数，通过Routh-Hurwitz准则分析参数化模型失配下的稳定性，并提出了鲁棒性和性能导向的两种调参方法。结果表明作动器滞后和惯性失配影响较小，而控制效能失配（特别是符号错误）是最危险的失稳因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管INDI控制对倾转旋翼VTOL无人机具有吸引力，但快速内环可容许的模型失配范围尚未以参数显式的方式进行解析刻画，缺乏对稳定边界的系统性分析。

Method: 隔离级联INDI控制器中的俯仰速率/升降副翼子通道，推导控制器-估计器-作动器-被控对象互联的五阶闭环传递函数，利用Routh-Hurwitz准则分析参数化线性模型下的稳定性，并基于三维参数扫描生成可解释的稳定区域。

Result: 得到了可解释的稳定区域图；发现作动器滞后和惯性失配在标称增益下影响较小，而控制效能失配（尤其是符号错误）是最危险的失稳因素；提出了鲁棒性导向和性能导向两种调参策略。

Conclusion: 基于稳定性区域图，为保守和激进两种运行条件给出了具体的调参建议，为倾转旋翼VTOL的INDI控制器设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) is attractive for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight control because it reduces dependence on a full aerodynamic model while retaining strong disturbance-rejection capability. For a tilt-rotor vertical takeoff and landing (VTOL) architecture, however, the admissible model-mismatch range of the fast inner loop is still not characterized analytically in a parameter-explicit way. This paper isolates the pitch-rate/elevon subchannel of an existing cascaded INDI controller and studies its linear stability under model mismatch. A closed-form fifth-order transfer function is derived for the full controller-estimator-actuator-plant interconnection, and stability is characterized through the Routh-Hurwitz criterion over a parameterized linear model. Two representative three-parameter sweeps produce interpretable stability regions. Based on these feasibility maps, two uncertainty-aware tuning procedures are proposed: a robustness-oriented design that maximizes a weighted worst-case combination of gain margin and phase margin, and a performance-oriented design that maximizes worst-case closed-loop bandwidth subject to margin constraints. The results show that actuator lag and inertia mismatch are comparatively benign at nominal gain, whereas control-effectiveness mismatch, particularly a sign error in the allocation, is the most dangerous destabilizing factor, leading to concrete tuning recommendations for conservative and aggressive operating conditions.

</details>


### [199] [Certifiable Safe Model-Based Reinforcement Learning with Control-Affine Dynamics Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16501)
*Hao Zhou,Yanze Zhang,Cameron Reid,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全强化学习框架，利用控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）对机器人动力学进行控制仿射建模，并通过自适应共形预测（ACP）量化安全约束的不确定性，结合控制障碍函数（CBF）实现数据驱动的安全策略，在cartpole和3D四旋翼仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常忽略残差模型不确定性中的结构（如与控制输入仿射相关的分量），导致行为过于保守或安全保证无效，亟需一种能利用控制仿射结构的数据驱动安全策略框架。

Method: 1. 使用控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）建模动力学，保持控制仿射形式，提升计算效率并减少模型偏差；2. 应用自适应共形预测（ACP）进行无模型不确定性量化，评估控制仿射动力学的安全约束不确定性；3. 结合控制障碍函数（CBF）进行可证明安全的控制器合成。

Result: 在cartpole和3D四旋翼仿真平台上验证了所提框架的有效性，表明该方法能够在保障安全的同时提升任务效率。

Conclusion: 利用控制仿射结构进行数据驱动建模，结合ACP不确定性量化，可有效解决安全强化学习中的保守性问题，并为基于CBF的控制器提供可证明的安全保证。

Abstract: Safe model-based reinforcement learning (RL) often bridges control-theoretic analysis and RL for robots to safely explore (partially) unknown system dynamics while deriving control actions for task efficiency. The control performance and safety assurance typically rely on prior knowledge of partially modeled nominal system dynamics and the data-driven models that compensate for residual model uncertainties. However, existing methods often overlook the structure of residual model uncertainties (e.g., components affine in control), which could lead to overly conservative robot behaviors or invalid safety guarantees under the safe learning-based controllers. This paper proposes a safe reinforcement learning framework that learns control-affine dynamics with a certifiable data-driven safe policy using control barrier functions (CBF). Specifically, we first use Control-Affine Random Fourier Features (ARFF) to model robot dynamics in a control-affine form, which offers computational efficiency that scales with dataset size and reduces potential model bias for model-based reinforcement learning. Then, a model-free, efficient uncertainty quantification method using adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) is applied to quantify the uncertainty in the safety constraint arising from the learned control-affine dynamics. This allows for data-driven safety assurance amenable to principled and efficient controller synthesis with CBF. Simulation results on the cartpole and the 3D quadrotor platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [200] [Foresight Residual RL for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16506)
*Yuhan Liu,Xinyu Zhang,Litao Liu,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Foresight Residual RL方法，通过为每个子任务的稀疏成功奖励添加离线估计的前瞻价值（即当前子任务终止状态下游子任务成功的概率），优化VLA策略在紧密公差装配任务中的交接质量。在Isaac Gym的三阶段螺母拧紧任务中达到85.6%的成功率，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在紧密公差、接触丰富的装配任务中常因长程信用分配和子任务耦合而失败：当前技能几何上成功的状态可能对下游技能是脆弱的。标准残差RL改进每个子任务孤立表现，但链式执行时收益甚微。

Method: (1) 训练一个视觉前瞻预测器，从基础策略的终止状态图像中预测下游子任务成功概率；(2) 通过后向前瞻归纳训练残差策略，将预测器输出作为奖励乘数，优化子任务间交接质量。

Result: 在Isaac Gym三阶段螺母拧紧任务（抓取-移动插入-旋转）中达到85.6%完整任务成功率，优于标准子任务残差RL（54.5%）和VLA基线，且各子任务成功率保持不变。

Conclusion: 提升长程任务性能的关键在于塑造每个子任务产生的成功状态的质量（即对下游子任务的友好程度），而不仅仅是确保子任务成功与否。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies offer strong general-purpose manipulation priors, but often fail on tight-tolerance, contact-rich assembly due to long-horizon credit assignment and subtask coupling: a state that is geometrically successful for the current skill can be brittle for downstream skills. We show this failure mode in residual reinforcement learning (RL) over a frozen VLA base policy: constant sparse success rewards improve each subtask in isolation yet yield little or no gain when skills are chained, because terminal state quality is uncontrolled. We propose Foresight Residual RL, which optimizes handoff quality by augmenting each subtask's sparse success reward with an offline-estimated foresight value -- the probability of future subtask success conditioned on the terminal state of the current subtask. Concretely, we (i) train a visual foresight predictor from images of terminal states of the base policy, labeled using downstream rollout statistics, and (ii) train residual policies via backward foresight induction, using the predictor output as a reward multiplier. On a three-phase wrench-based nut-tightening assembly task in Isaac Gym (grasp, move-insert, rotate), our method achieves 85.6% full-task success, outperforming standard subtask residual RL (54.5%) and VLA baselines, while leaving per-subtask success unchanged. These results highlight that improving long-horizon performance requires shaping which successful states are produced at each sub-task, not only whether success occurs.

</details>


### [201] [Differentiable Reinforcement Learning for Path Tracking by an Agile Fish-Like Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16508)
*Prashanth Chivkula,Kartik Loya,Venkata Ravindhra Reddy Varikuti,Phanindra Tallapragada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对仿生鱼机器人的控制难题，开发了高效近似的仿真平台，并利用基于时间反向传播的PID控制与课程学习训练控制策略，成功实现了仿真到实物的迁移，且匹配度极佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿生鱼机器人虽受广泛研究，但由于流固耦合建模困难及非线性欠驱动特性，其控制与运动规划极具挑战；现有仿真环境在速度与精度上难以满足强化学习训练需求，阻碍了该领域进展。

Method: 构建计算高效的近似仿真平台；采用PID控制（变增益），通过时间反向传播（BPTT）进行增益学习，并引入课程学习策略进行训练；最后将仿真学习的策略直接部署到物理机器人上。

Result: 仿真中习得的控制策略直接应用于物理平台，表现出极佳的匹配度，证明仿真到实物的成功迁移。

Conclusion: 所提出的高效仿真平台与基于BPTT的PID增益学习方法能有效解决仿生鱼机器人的控制难题，并具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Fish-like swimming has inspired the design of several dozens if not hundreds of bioinspired robots in the last few decades. But the control and motion planning of such robots has been challenging due to the poorly modeled fluid-structure interaction and the nonlinear underactuated dynamics of such robots. While reinforcement learning has allowed significant advances in the context of ground and aerial robots, the lack of a suitable simulation environment with appropriate computational speed and accuracy have prevented similar progress for fish-like robots. We address this two-fold problem by developing a simulation platform that approximates the motion of our fish-like robot with computational efficiency. Then the motion control and path tracking by the robot is performed using PID control where the (variable) gains are learned using back propagation through time and training on a curriculum. The policy learned in the simulation is then applied on the physical platform, demonstrating an excellent match.

</details>


### [202] [Autonomous VR-Based Risk Detection for Situational Awareness in Dangerous Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16582)
*Mohammad Eskandari,Murali Krishna Varma Indukuri,Stephanie M. Lukin,Cynthia Matuszek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于VR的人机交互框架，让配备VLM的机器人在危险环境中识别并标注风险，通过VR界面传递给操作员。实验表明，带标注的VR界面比无标注基线更受用户欢迎，参与者认为系统清晰、有用且舒适。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾害响应等高危场景中，情景感知不仅依赖检测危险，还需要向人类操作员清晰传达危险信息。VLM在安全关键场景的理解上表现优异，但其作为面向人类的机器人系统组成部分的价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了一个VR-based人机交互框架：机器人探索虚拟场景→调用VLM识别潜在危险→在用户视角标注兴趣点→通过沉浸式VR界面呈现给人类操作员。该框架支持对机器人危险识别和危险信息传达的双重可控评估。

Result: 用户更偏好带标注的VR界面（相比无标注基线）；参与者对系统的清晰度、有用性和舒适度均给出了高评价。

Conclusion: 将VLM驱动的机器人感知与沉浸式可视化结合，是支持危险环境中情景感知的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: In high-risk environments such as disaster response, situational awareness depends not only on detecting hazards but also on communicating them clearly to human operators. Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for scene understanding in safety-critical settings, yet their value as part of human-facing robotic systems remains underexplored. We present a VR-based Human Robot Interaction framework for studying how VLM-assisted robots can support situational awareness in simulated hazardous environments. In our system, a robot explores a virtual scene and queries a VLM to identify potential hazards and annotate user-facing points of interest. These annotations are presented to a human operator through an immersive VR interface. This framework enables controlled evaluation of both robotic hazard identification and the communication of safety-critical information to users. Results from our study indicate that the annotated VR interface was preferred over the unannotated baseline and that participants reported high clarity, usefulness, and comfort when interacting with the system. These findings suggest that combining VLM-based robotic perception with immersive visualization is a promising approach for supporting situational awareness in hazardous settings.

</details>


### [203] [An Indoor Navigation System for the Visually Impaired based on UWB Positioning and D* Lite Path Planning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16614)
*Thanh C. Vo,Dong LT. Tran,Huy HM. Le,Duyen N Ha,Tuan Anh Pham,Hai Thanh Dang,Hoang T. Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于UWB定位和D* Lite算法的室内导航系统，用于帮助视障人士在GPS受限环境中实现精准定位和动态路径规划。实验表明系统具有低延迟、高可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视障人士在GPS信号缺失的室内环境中难以精准定位和导航的问题，提升其独立行动的安全性和灵活性。

Method: 采用UWB（超宽带）传感器实现室内高精度定位，并集成D* Lite路径规划算法，支持在动态障碍物出现时快速重新规划路径。

Result: 系统在复杂室内空间中运行可靠，延迟低，能够安全、灵活地引导用户。

Conclusion: 该导航系统有效弥补了GPS在室内环境的不足，为视障人士提供了实用的导航解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes an indoor navigation system for the visually impaired, leveraging Ultra-Wideband (UWB) positioning technology and the D*Lite path planning algorithm. The system utilizes UWB sensors to provide precision localization in GPS-denied environments. The D* Lite algorithm is integrated to optimize travel trajectories and ensure rapid route re-planning in the presence of dynamic obstacles. Experimental results demonstrate that the system operates reliably with low latency, providing safety and flexibility for users in complex indoor spaces.

</details>


### [204] [SAGE: A Socially-Aware Generative Engine for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16619)
*Lan Hu,Minghui Liwang,Wenbo Zhu,Xinlei Yi,Yiguang Hong,Xianbin Wang,Zhenzhen Jiao,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE是一个面向异构多智能体导航的社交感知生成引擎，利用异构图Transformer和扩散生成模型联合建模轨迹预测与规划，并通过训练自由的能量引导机制优化安全性和社交合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理异构参与者（不同动态、自主性和社会角色）的交互时，过度依赖同质性假设或仅考虑几何碰撞约束，难以联合建模不对称交互、耦合预测-规划和软性社会规范。

Method: 1) 将有向异构图作为场景表示，使用HGT编码类型特定的不对称交互；2) 基于上下文，采用扩散生成模块联合建模未来轨迹与机器人规划；3) 推理时通过训练免费的安全-社交能量引导机制，利用可微碰撞、运动学、任务进度和角色条件社交合规项细化采样轨迹。

Result: 在ETH/UCY、SDD及合成数据集上验证了有效性，在提升安全性和社交合规性的同时保持任务性能；引导机制持续降低碰撞率和社交违规率，可扩展至20台机器人，无需重新训练即可控制安全-精度-任务权衡。

Conclusion: SAGE是复杂环境中社交感知多智能体导航的可扩展框架，证明了异构建模与扩散引导结合的有效性。

Abstract: Safe and socially compliant navigation in open human-robot environments requires robots to reason about heterogeneous participants with different dynamics, autonomy levels, and social roles. Existing trajectory prediction and planning methods often rely on homogeneous interaction assumptions or enforce only geometric collision constraints, making it difficult to jointly model asymmetric interactions, coupled prediction-planning, and soft social norms. This paper proposes SAGE, a socially-aware generative engine for heterogeneous multi-agent navigation. SAGE represents robots and surrounding entities as a directed heterogeneous graph and employs a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT) to encode type-specific asymmetric interactions. Conditioned on the resulting context, a diffusion-based generative module jointly models future entity trajectories and robot trajectory plans. During inference, a training-free safety-social energy guidance mechanism refines sampled robot trajectories using differentiable collision, kinematic, task-progress, and role-conditioned social-compliance terms. Extensive experiments on real-world (ETH/UCY and SDD) and synthetic datasets verify the effectiveness of SAGE in improving safety and social compliance while maintaining task performance. The proposed guidance mechanism consistently reduces collision and social-violation rates, scales to teams of up to 20 robots, and enables explicit control of the safety-accuracy-task trade-off without retraining. These findings demonstrate the potential of SAGE as a scalable framework for socially-aware multi-agent navigation in complex environments.

</details>


### [205] [AI-Augmented Model Predictive Control for Safe and Adaptive Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16630)
*Luca Sportelli,Tyler Barr,Cagri Kilic,Di Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于多智能体对抗场景下的自主航天器交会与接近操作（RPO）。该框架结合了约束滚动时域MPC与数据驱动监督调优层，在Kerbal Space Program Differential Game环境中通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证，相比固定参数MPC在鲁棒性、自适应机动能力和交会性能方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于学习的方法在航天器自主交会中展现出潜力，但其在安全关键轨道机器人应用中仍面临可解释性、鲁棒性和约束感知能力不足的问题，亟需一种兼具安全性、实时性和可解释性的制导架构。

Method: 提出自适应MPC框架：(1) 基于Clohessy-Wiltshire (CW)相对运动动力学构建约束滚动时域MPC，包含执行器限制、预测禁入区约束、松弛变量可行性处理及可选的CBF安全滤波；(2) 数据驱动监督调优层根据离线闭环评估和在线交互几何学调整可解释的MPC参数（如跟踪权重、安全惩罚、最小分离目标等）。

Result: 在KSPDG Capture-the-Satellite环境中通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证，自适应MPC在闭环鲁棒性、自适应机动行为和交会性能方面均优于固定参数MPC，同时保持了安全感知操作和实时可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个模块化、可解释的自适应航天器RPO基础框架，有效弥合了学习方法的灵活性与传统控制的可解释性、安全性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) in adversarial orbital environments require guidance architectures balancing target pursuit, safety preservation, and real-time adaptability under dynamically evolving interaction conditions. Although learning-based approaches show promise, their application to safety-critical orbital robotics remains limited by concerns regarding interpretability, robustness, and constraint awareness. This work presents an adaptive Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework for autonomous spacecraft RPO in multi-agent adversarial scenarios. The proposed architecture combines a constrained receding-horizon MPC formulation with a data-driven supervisory tuning layer that adjusts controller parameters from offline closed-loop evaluation and online interaction geometry. Relative motion follows Clohessy-Wiltshire (CW) dynamics, enabling computationally efficient finite-horizon prediction and real-time quadratic optimization. The MPC formulation incorporates actuator limits, predictive keep-out-zone constraints, slack-variable feasibility handling, and optional Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety filtering. Rather than generating thrust commands directly, the adaptive layer modifies interpretable MPC parameters, including tracking weights, safety penalties, minimum-separation objectives, and keep-out-zone objectives. The framework was evaluated in the official Kerbal Space Program Differential Game (KSPDG) Capture-the-Satellite environment through Monte Carlo simulations. Results demonstrate improved closed-loop robustness, adaptive maneuvering behavior, and rendezvous performance compared with fixed-parameter MPC while preserving safety-aware operation and real-time feasibility, providing a modular, interpretable foundation for adaptive spacecraft RPO.

</details>


### [206] [PhyAgentOS: A Self-Evolving Operating System for Embodied Agents with Decoupled Cognitive Planning and Physical Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16636)
*Yang Liu,Weixing Chen,Xinshuai Song,Tao Pu,Siwen Mo,Yongjie Bai,Zihao Chen,Qianran Sun,Liruo Zhong,Ying Shen,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyAgentOS 是一个运行时基础架构，将调度、验证、记忆、基准测试和安全作为系统级服务，通过会话中心运行时、状态即文件协议和会话验证器等机制，实现视觉-语言-动作模型、世界模型和智能体规划器的统一组合与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型、世界模型和智能体规划器缺乏统一的执行抽象、共享状态、语义验证机制以及跨异构本体的持久化经验，导致三者难以有效组合。

Method: 提出PhyAgentOS，核心创新包括：1) Session-Centered Runtime，将会话而非动作作为最小调度单元；2) State-as-a-File协议，以Markdown+YAML格式将跨层状态物化为文件系统，实现认知与物理的解耦；3) SessionVerifier，通过基于证据的判定区分执行终止和语义任务完成；4) 认知记忆机制，将验证结果转化为可复用的知识；5) 分层安全机制，覆盖预检、动作桥接、安全守护、心跳监控和目标约束。

Result: 在Optimus-67、StarDojo和DST-Dojo基准上评测，在19+种模拟和物理本体上验证，在LIBERO、Calvin和RoboCasa365上跨多个VLA模型取得性能提升。

Conclusion: PhyAgentOS为物理智能提供了一个系统级的运行时基础，通过统一的会话抽象、文件系统级状态接口和证据驱动的验证闭环，有效解决了异构模型组合执行中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models, world models, and agentic planners each advance physical intelligence, yet their composition lacks a common execution abstraction, shared state, semantic verification, and persistent experience across heterogeneous embodiments. We present PhyAgentOS, a runtime foundation delivering scheduling, verification, memory, benchmarking, and safety as system-level services. Its Session-Centered Runtime treats a session, not an action, as the minimum unit of scheduling, compatibility preflight, supervised execution, evidence collection, and acceptance. To decouple cognition from physical execution, the cognition-physics boundary is a file system: the State-as-a-File protocol materializes cross-layer state as Markdown with YAML, yielding inspectable, versionable records without code dependencies between Agent and Runtime layers. These views form a unified cognitive state space aligning intent, capabilities, environment, execution, and experience. The SessionVerifier distinguishes execution termination from semantic task completion via evidence-grounded verdicts of success, failure, or replan. Verified outcomes are consolidated through epistemic memory into reusable knowledge and corrective lessons, closing a trial-and-error loop without retraining. Benchmarking reuses the deployment session and verification path, so results trace to real execution. Layered safety constrains both policy-driven and agent-driven execution: preflight, action bridges, SafetyGuard, heartbeat monitoring, and target-local constraints. Validation is progressive: games test cognitive planning, simulation adds dynamics and control, real robots add hardware noise, with the cognitive layer held constant. PhyAgentOS is benchmarked on Optimus-67, StarDojo, and DST-Dojo, validated on 19+ simulated and physical embodiments, and gains on LIBERO, Calvin, and RoboCasa365 across multiple VLA models.

</details>


### [207] [Token-Wise Latent Streaming from Slow Reasoners to Fast Planners for Dynamic Vision Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16806)
*Tianshuai Hu,Yangyi Zhong,Zeying Gong,Lingdong Kong,Xiaodong Mei,Guoyang Zhao,Xiaolu Liu,Song Wang,Rong Li,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARK-VLN，一种双系统框架，通过在VLM推理过程中流式传输中间隐状态给快速流匹配专家规划器，解决了动态社交VLN中语言推理慢与安全规划需即时响应之间的矛盾，在保持推理效率的同时提升了导航成功率和社交合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人类环境中的视觉-语言导航面临根本矛盾：语言推理缓慢谨慎，而安全合规的社交规划需要即时反应。推理过程中的观测过时问题可能导致选择的动作在执行时已不安全。

Method: 提出SPARK-VLN双系统框架，包含三个模块：Token-Wise Hidden Streamer（逐token提取中间隐状态）、Sequence-to-Slot Latent Bridge（将隐状态投影为固定尺寸潜变量槽）、Evolving Latent Conditioner（将潜变量注入专家规划器），实现慢速VLM推理器向快速流匹配专家规划器的知识流式传输。

Result: 在动态社交VLN基准测试中，SPARK-VLN在保持推理效率的同时，提升了导航成功率和社交合规性，减少了人类碰撞和观测过时统计量。

Conclusion: VLM的中间隐状态在推理完成前就已编码了动作相关的意图，通过流式传输这些信息可以在不牺牲推理效率的前提下实现安全、社交合规的导航规划。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in dynamic, human-centric environments exposes a fundamental tension: linguistic reasoning is slow and deliberative, whereas safe, socially compliant planning should be instant and reactive. The resulting observation staleness is safety-critical: a maneuver chosen during inference can already be unsafe by the time it executes. We observe that, long before a VLM finishes its inference, its intermediate hidden states already encode action-relevant intent. We propose SPARK-VLN, a dual-system framework for dynamic social VLN that streams the slow VLM reasoner's knowledge to a fast flow-matching expert planner throughout token generation, providing fresh and evolving guidance during inference. This design is realized by three modules: a Token-Wise Hidden Streamer that extracts intermediate hidden states along the token generation process, a Sequence-to-Slot Latent Bridge that projects them into fixed-size latent slots, and an Evolving Latent Conditioner that infuses them into the expert planner. We also introduce a human-centric benchmark suite for dynamic social vision-language navigation that keeps pedestrians and the robot active throughout inference and reports navigation success, social compliance, human collisions, and explicit staleness statistics. Across these settings, SPARK-VLN mproves navigation success and social compliance while sustaining inference efficiency. Webpage: https://hutslib.github.io/SPARK-VLN/.

</details>


### [208] [GLidE-SLAM: GL-Accelerated Indirect-Direct Embedded SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16897)
*Carlos A. Pinheiro de Sousa,Heiko Hamann,Oliver Deussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLidE-SLAM提出了一种混合间接-直接单目视觉SLAM框架，通过GPU加速直接跟踪（利用OpenGL ES 3.1计算着色器）处理中间帧，保留完整间接管道用于地图扩展和全局一致性，在嵌入式设备上实现高达9倍帧率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
