The practical applicability of TSC-VP-STO is validated through real-world deployment on a forestry crane, including a full log-loading cycle.

</details>


### [245] [Receiver-Centered Robot-to-Human Handover with Grasp-Aware Object Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17839)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语音驱动、以接收者为中心的协作机器人自适应工具交接系统，利用LLM进行意图识别、MediaPipe进行3D手部追踪，动态调整机械臂末端姿态以提供符合人体工学的握持姿势。实验表明，该系统相比静态基线方法减少了非对称工具的抓取延迟，并提升了信任感知和任务流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人机工具交接多采用静态方式，在处理非对称工业工具时，机器人往往以固定姿态递送工具，导致操作者需要调整手部姿势或重新抓取，造成延迟和不适。本文旨在解决这一问题，通过接收者中心的自适应策略提升交接的自然性和安全性。

Method: 基于Franka协作机器人平台，集成LLM进行语音指令的意图理解，结合MediaPipe实现实时3D手部追踪，系统据此动态调整机器人末端执行器的朝向，以最优的"手柄朝前"姿态递送工具。采用被试内设计，比较自适应系统与对象无关的静态基线方法的表现。

Result: 自适应系统显著减少了非对称工具交接中的抓取延迟，提升了交互流畅性；同时在主观信任感知方面，特别是运动可预测性和感知任务简单性上有显著改善。

Conclusion: 以接收者为中心的自适应交接策略能够有效提升人机协作效率与信任体验，特别适用于非对称工业工具的交接场景，为未来智能协作机器人的交互设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly sharing workspaces with human operators, making tool handover a frequent and safety-critical micro-interaction. However, traditional static handovers often lead to awkward grasps when handling asymmetric industrial tools. This paper presents a receiver-centered voice-driven adaptive handover system for mechanical tools, built on a Franka cobot. Using an LLM for intention recognition and MediaPipe for real-time 3D hand tracking, the framework dynamically adjusts the end-effector's orientation to present tools in an ergonomically optimal, handle-first pose. A within-subjects study compared this adaptive approach with an object-agnostic static baseline. The results demonstrate that the adaptive system reduces the grasp delay for asymmetric tools, improving the fluency of the interaction. Furthermore, the adaptive strategy improved specific trust-related perceptions, particularly motion predictability and perceived task simplicity.

</details>


### [246] [Lifelong Localization in Dynamic Indoor Environments Combining Odometry with Sparse Distance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17852)
*Michael M. Bilevich,Tomer Buber,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏距离采样的鲁棒终身定位框架，仅用少量距离样本即可在动态室内环境中实现与SLAM相当的定位效果，且能实时解决绑架机器人问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在实际动态环境中，预建地图因动态障碍物变得不准确，现有定位技术无法有效应对。同时，传统方法依赖全量LiDAR数据，成本高、隐私性差、带宽需求大，需要更经济高效的定位方案。

Method: 利用机器人里程计和稀疏距离采样获取位置先验，融合时间信息收敛到真实位姿。先验基于距离样本提供鲁棒的位置估计，可实时解决绑架机器人问题；再随时间与里程计融合，逐步收敛。方法在静态环境下有收敛保证，在已学习的动态环境下同样成立。

Result: 在真实室内环境中，仅用16个距离样本即可达到与全量LiDAR SLAM相当的定位精度；可实时解决绑架机器人问题（至多存在对称性模糊）；在静态环境中有收敛性理论保证，动态环境在正确学习变化后同样保证收敛。

Conclusion: 稀疏距离采样作为一种低成本、高隐私、低带宽的替代方案，在动态室内环境中可实现鲁棒的终身定位，为实际机器人部署提供了实用且高效的新思路。

Abstract: Localization is a key task in robot navigation, and many techniques exist for it. In many plausible scenarios, a robot might face unforeseen, dynamic obstacles, rendering any pre-determined map inaccurate for localization. In this work, we propose a robust lifelong localization framework in dynamic planar indoor environments, using the robot's odometry and sparse distance sampling. We demonstrate how distance samples can be used to provide a robust prior on the robot's location. This technique can solve the kidnapped robot problem in real time, up to symmetries. Based on insights from real-world recorded data, we also account for dynamic obstacles. We then fuse this prior, over time, with the odometry to converge to the robot's location. A central property of our method is that it provably converges to the robot's ground truth pose even in large indoor environments when the environment is static. We further show that this guarantee also holds in dynamic environments, as long as the nature of those changes has been correctly learned. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in different real-world indoor environments. In particular, we achieve a localization comparable to SLAM with merely a few (sixteen) distance samples, as opposed to the full LiDAR range. Sufficing with only sparse distance sampling is advantageous in terms of sensor cost, privacy, storage space, and transmission bandwidth.

</details>


### [247] [ConceptTree: Bringing Semantic Transparency to Black-Box Decision Making for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17861)
*Yongyan Wen,Feifan Liu,Jinyi Chen,Bo An,Peng Liu,Siyuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ConceptTree框架，通过将高层操作技能选择建模为可解释概念空间上的决策树推理，实现长序列机器人操作的透明决策过程，支持细粒度干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法将技能选择视为从观察到动作的黑箱映射，缺乏决策过程的透明性，难以实现可靠的人类监督和干预。

Method: 学习一个基于视觉输入归一化的概念空间，在该空间上训练决策树来预测高层技能，将策略表示为视觉观察上的概念级谓词序列。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，ConceptTree持续优于现有基于概念的基线方法，尤其在复杂长序列场景中表现更佳。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可追溯、可干预的透明决策过程，支持通过修改单个概念进行细粒度纠错，无需重新训练。

Abstract: Establishing interpretable decision-making processes in long-horizon robotic manipulation is critical for enabling reliable human oversight and intervention. However, existing approaches to robotic manipulation largely treat skill selection as opaque mappings from observations to actions, offering limited transparency into how decisions are formed. In this work, we propose ConceptTree, a framework that reframes high-level manipulation skill selection as reasoning over human-interpretable concepts, representing high-level policies as a sequence of concept-level predicates over visual observations. Rather than relying on implicit latent representations, our method learns a normalized concept space grounded in visual inputs, over which a decision tree is trained to predict high-level skills. This formulation yields a transparent decision process that is both traceable and intervenable, enabling direct inspection and modification of policy behavior. We evaluate our approach on a set of real-world robotic manipulation tasks with increasing complexity. Experimental results show that ConceptTree consistently outperforms existing concept-based baselines, particularly in complex, long-horizon scenarios. Furthermore, we provide qualitative case studies showing that our model supports fine-grained intervention by modifying individual concepts, enabling targeted correction of decision errors without retraining.

</details>


### [248] [Leveraging Two Robotic Arms for Tight Assembly Performance Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17876)
*Dror Livnat,Yuval Lavi,Michael M. Bilevich,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用双机械臂协同完成装配任务的全流程框架，相比单臂系统，平均执行时间减少50%以上，轨迹质量更高，搜索有效机器人放置更快，并提供理论保证和开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单臂装配在紧配合操作中存在效率低、轨迹质量有限等问题，作者希望利用双臂协同的优势提升装配操作的执行效率和轨迹质量，并提供理论上的执行时间与轨迹精度保证。

Method: 提出一个端到端框架，输入为零件CAD模型及其装配状态下的相对位置，输出为双机械臂的协同装配执行方案。方法在仿真和物理机器人上均实现，且提供了关于执行时间、轨迹精度的理论保证。

Result: 双臂协同相比单臂系统平均执行时间减少50%以上；产生更高质量的轨迹；加速了有效机器人放置位置的搜索；建立了机器人工作单元所需尺寸的边界。

Conclusion: 双臂协同在紧配合装配操作中优于单臂系统，具有显著的时间、质量和搜索效率优势，且方法具有可复现性（开源代码和实物演示）。

Abstract: We provide a novel end-to-end framework for the execution of an assembly operation by two robotic arms, given the digital CAD models of the parts and their desired relative placement in their assembled state. We analyze and demonstrate the advantages of using two robotic arms simultaneously in tight assembly operations, compared to single-arm systems. Our method is implemented in both simulation and using physical robots. It provides theoretical guarantees on execution time and trajectory accuracy, supported by empirical evidence. In particular, we show that coordinated movement of two arms reduces average execution time by more than 50% compared to using a single arm only, produces higher-quality trajectories, and accelerates the search for valid robot placements. Furthermore, we establish bounds on the required dimensions of the robotic cell. Our open source software together with real-life video demonstrations are available in our project page.

</details>


### [249] [Manifold-Guided Motion Planning for Tight Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17898)
*Dror Livnat,Michael M. Bilevich,Michal Kleinbort,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CMG-RRT算法，通过将采样偏向于临界流形（零件间至少有一个接触点的构型空间子集），有效解决刚性体装配中的紧约束运动规划问题，在旋转装配基准测试中达到100%成功率，并首次自动求解了Elk解谜难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性体装配运动规划面临紧几何约束的挑战，可行路径往往需要通过几乎零间隙的构型，现有方法难以高效处理此类问题。

Method: 提出临界流形引导的RRT算法（CMG-RRT），利用层次化子空间划分，自适应地将采样偏向于临界流形（至少有一个接触点的构型空间子集）的邻域。

Result: 在挑战性的旋转装配基准测试中达到100%成功率，并首次完全自动化求解了Elk解谜难题。

Conclusion: CMG-RRT在标准间隙假设下具有概率完备性，为紧约束装配问题提供了一种高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Motion planning for rigid-body assembly poses a fundamental challenge in robotics due to tight geometric constraints. In such scenarios, feasible motions often require passing through (near-)zero clearance configurations in which the parts are tightly constrained by contact. In this work, we introduce Critical-Manifold Guided RRT (CMG-RRT), a sampling-based planner designed specifically for tight assembly problems. Our key observation is that in tight assemblies, valid solution paths lie on or near a critical manifold: the subset of configuration space consisting of poses with at least one contact point between parts. CMG-RRT guides exploration by adaptively biasing sampling toward neighborhoods of the critical manifold using a hierarchical subdivision of the configuration space. We prove that CMG-RRT is probabilistically complete under standard clearance assumptions. Empirical evaluation on challenging rotational assembly benchmarks demonstrates a 100% success rate across all tested instances, including, to the best of our knowledge, the first fully automatic solution of the Elk disentanglement puzzle. Our open source software is available through our project page: https://www.cgl.cs.tau.ac.il/projects/tight-assembly-planning.

</details>


### [250] [UMCP: A Unified Multi-Task Collaborative Perception Network for Luggage Trolley Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17950)
*Zhirui Sun,Zhihao Jiang,Yao Wang,Jianwei Peng,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一多任务协同感知网络(UMCP)，基于YOLOv12架构同时进行行李车检测、关键点检测和方向估计，通过方向特征增强模块和KL散度损失提升方向估计精度，在降低模型复杂度和计算成本的同时实现竞争性精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行李车视觉感知方法依赖级联多模型推理，导致推理延迟高、部署成本大，难以满足机器人自主收集行李车在杂乱动态环境中对高精度和实时性的双重要求。

Method: 基于YOLOv12架构，构建多任务协同感知网络(UMCP)，融合关键点特征与方向特征后输入方向特征增强模块(OFEM)，并采用圆形概率分布建模与KL散度损失优化方向估计。

Result: 与现有方法相比，UMCP在保持竞争性整体精度的同时，显著降低了模型复杂度和计算开销。

Conclusion: UMCP通过统一的多任务协同框架有效解决了传统级联方法的效率瓶颈，为机器人自主行李车收集提供了一种高效、低成本的视觉感知方案。

Abstract: In robotic autonomous luggage trolley collection, robots must continuously localize scattered luggage trolleys in cluttered and dynamic environments. This requires the vision system to achieve both high accuracy and real-time performance. However, existing visual perception approaches for luggage trolleys often rely on cascaded multi-model inference, leading to increased inference latency and high deployment costs. To address these limitations, this article presents a unified multi-task collaborative perception network (UMCP) that simultaneously performs luggage trolley detection, keypoint detection and orientation estimation. Based on the YOLOv12 architecture, keypoint features are fused with orientation features and then fed into an orientation feature enhancement module (OFEM), thereby improving orientation estimation accuracy. In addition, circular probability distribution modeling with a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence loss is adopted to enhance orientation estimation accuracy further. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive overall accuracy while substantially reducing model complexity and computational cost compared with existing methods. A website about this work is available at https://sites.google.com/view/robot-umcp.

</details>


### [251] [Does Robust VIO Need More Learning? Geometry-Verified Visual Measurements under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17956)
*Yangyang Ning,Shu Liang,Quanbo Ge,Tianchen Deng,Yuhua Qi,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出最小化学习的立体VIO框架，仅用SEA-RAFT生成密集立体对应点及不确定性，其余环节保持显式几何与状态估计。实验表明，在分布偏移下，精心集成的学习视觉测量比学习更多管线更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将学习引入VIO全流程的方法在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足，作者质疑是否真的需要深度学习的全管线估计，还是将学习限定在视觉测量生成即可。

Method: 提出最小化学习的立体VIO框架：SEA-RAFT仅用于生成密集立体对应点并预测不确定性；时间跟踪、几何验证、状态估计保持显式。密集光流在稀疏特征点采样，经预测不确定性和立体极线一致性滤波后，通过不确定性加权的重投影因子融入滑窗立体惯性估计器。不确定性进一步传播到立体三角化，用于下游各向异性3D高斯建图。

Result: 在EuRoC、VIODE、4Seasons数据集上，在运动模糊、动态场景、光照变化及室内外分布偏移下均实现准确稳定估计。消融实验表明，单独学习光流不足，增益来自学习对应点与几何验证、不确定性加权的结合。

Conclusion: 对于分布偏移鲁棒的VIO，精心集成的学习视觉测量比学习更大比例的估计管线更有效。

Abstract: Learning is increasingly introduced into visual-inertial odometry (VIO), ranging from learned feature front-ends to learning-dominant motion and geometry estimation. However, learning more of the pipeline does not necessarily improve robustness when deployment conditions differ from the training distribution. This work asks whether robust VIO under distribution shift truly requires deeper learned estimation, or whether learning can be confined to visual measurement generation. We propose a minimal-learning stereo VIO framework in which SEA-RAFT is used only to propose dense stereo correspondences and predict their uncertainty, while temporal tracking, geometric verification, and state estimation remain explicit. Dense flow is sampled at sparse feature locations, filtered using predicted uncertainty and stereo epipolar consistency, and incorporated into a sliding-window stereo-inertial estimator through uncertainty-weighted reprojection factors. The same uncertainty is further propagated through stereo triangulation for downstream anisotropic 3D Gaussian mapping. Experiments on EuRoC, VIODE, and 4Seasons demonstrate accurate and stable estimation under motion blur, dynamic scenes, illumination changes, and large indoor-to-outdoor distribution shifts. Ablations show that learned flow alone is insufficient: the gains arise from combining learned correspondence proposals with geometric verification and uncertainty-aware weighting. These results suggest that, for OOD-robust VIO, carefully integrated learned visual measurements can be more effective than learning a larger fraction of the estimation pipeline. Code and configs for the benchmark will be open-source upon acceptance. A supplementary video is available at https://drive.google.com/file/d/1EVRhOkhanmNXHbQS1Vr80FoEIAYOYOV2/view

</details>


### [252] [MEVION: Low-Cost Open-Source Data Collection System for Powerful and High-Speed Dual-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17970)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Yoshiki Obinata,Hirokazu Ishida,Jihoon Oh,Temma Suzuki,Shintaro Inoue,Keita Yoneda,Ayumu Iwata,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEVION，一种低成本、开源的双臂机器人数据采集系统，通过使用闭链机构和钣金焊接等设计，在约14,000美元的预算下实现了更大力量（每臂最大扭矩60Nm）和速度的操控能力，支持原本难以完成的物体操作任务数据采集和模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ALOHA系统虽然低成本且开源，但力量和速度有限，难以处理重物或快速操作。研究者希望开发一种同样低成本但能产生更大力和速度的双臂机器人数据采集系统，以扩展可研究的任务范围。

Method: MEVION采用四个6自由度臂（每个重7.0kg，最大扭矩60Nm），肘关节使用类似四足机器人的闭链机构以减少远端质量，主体结构大量采用钣金焊接以降低成本并简化组装，所有部件均可通过电商采购，总成本约14,000美元。

Result: 成功实现了对原本ALOHA系统无法完成的物体操作任务的数据采集，并支持基于模仿学习的运动生成。该系统所有软硬件已开源。

Conclusion: MEVION证明了在低成本、开源的前提下，通过合理的设计（闭链机构、钣金焊接等）可以显著提升双臂机器人的力/速性能，拓展数据采集和模仿学习可覆盖的任务范围，为机器人基础模型的数据收集提供了新的实用方案。

Abstract: The global competition for developing robotic foundation models is intensifying. Among the data collection systems used for dual-arm robots, ALOHA is representative of being low-cost and open-source, and is widely adopted by researchers as a de facto standard. However, due to its limited ability to generate high forces and speeds, it is difficult to handle heavy objects or perform fast manipulations. To address this, we developed MEVION, a low-cost and open-source dual-arm robot data collection system capable of generating greater force and speed. All parts of this robot can be sourced through e-commerce, and by extensively utilizing sheet metal welding, its large body structure is constructed with a small number of components at low cost, while also simplifying assembly. MEVION is equipped with four 6-DoF arms with parallel grippers. Each arm weighs 7.0 kg and has a maximum torque of 60 Nm, and the entire system can be constructed for about USD 14,000. The elbow joint adopts a closed-link mechanism similar to those used in quadruped robots, which reduces the distal mass and enables higher force and speed output at the end-effector. We demonstrate that MEVION enables data collection for object manipulation tasks not previously possible and supports imitation learning-based motion generation. All hardware and software of this work are included in the Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevion.

</details>


### [253] [RynnBrain 1.1: Towards More Capable and Generalizable Embodied Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17977)
*Kehan Li,Bohan Hou,Minghao Zhu,Tianyi Zhang,Zesen Cheng,Zhikai Wang,Sicong Leng,Xin Li,Xiao Lin,Biying Yao,Minghua Zeng,Jiangpin Liu,Ronghao Dang,Jiayan Guo,Siteng Huang,Haoyu Zhao,Heng Ping,Yaxi Zhao,Kexiang Wang,Tong Lu,Shengke Xue,Jiahao Tang,Yulei Wang,Zejing Wang,Jianwei Gao,Shijian Lu,Chengju Liu,Jianfei Yang,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnBrain 1.1 是一个系列化具身基础模型（2B、9B、122B-A10B），通过统一时空与物理框架训练，支持具身感知、空间推理、定位与规划。相比1.0版，新增接触点预测和原生3D定位能力，并在多实体机器人上部署VLA策略，在多个基准与实物实验中超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建更贴近机器人操作的具身基础模型，克服1.0版在3D空间理解与操作对齐方面的不足，并验证跨实体、多任务联合训练的有效性。

Method: 提出统一时空-物理嵌入框架；引入接触点预测与原生3D定位；设计统一跨实体动作空间与实体特定掩码的VLA架构；在Unitree G1、Astribot-S1、Tianji-Wuji三款机器人上部署验证。

Result: 在VSI-Bench、MMSI、RefSpatial-Bench上，122B-A10B模型超越所有评估的开源与闭源模型；实物实验中，RynnBrain初始化的策略优于Qwen基线和通用VLA；多任务+多实体联合训练相比单任务训练提升了流程分数与成功率。

Conclusion: RynnBrain 1.1 通过统一的物理-时空框架和跨实体VLA策略，显著提升了具身模型的3D理解与操作能力，验证了联合训练在多实体场景下的推广优势。

Abstract: We present RynnBrain 1.1, a family of embodied foundation models spanning 2B, 9B, and 122B-A10B scales. Trained with a unified spatio-temporal and physically grounded framework, RynnBrain 1.1 supports embodied perception, spatial reasoning, localization, and planning. Compared with RynnBrain 1.0, it further introduces contact-point prediction across the model family and native 3D grounding for the 2B and 9B models, yielding representations and outputs that are more directly aligned with robot manipulation. We also develop RynnBrain-VLA with a unified cross-embodiment action space and embodiment-specific masking, and deploy it on Unitree G1, Astribot-S1, and Tianji-Wuji. RynnBrain 1.1 achieves strong results on embodied cognition, localization, and 3D grounding, with the 122B-A10B model outperforming all evaluated proprietary and open-source models on VSI-Bench, MMSI, and RefSpatial-Bench. Real-robot experiments show that RynnBrain-initialized policies outperform Qwen-based and representative generalist VLAs, while joint multi-task and multi-embodiment training improves process scores and success rates over per-task training.

</details>


### [254] [Distilling Global Traversability Priors for Image-based Affordance Prediction in Off-road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17984)
*Matthew Sivaprakasam,Samuel Triest,Micah Nye,Deegan Atha,Shehryar Khattak,David Fan,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从第一人称视角图像中提取长距离可穿越前沿的方法，利用卫星图像生成训练数据，以解决传统自主导航方法在长距离非结构化地形中的短视问题，在离线基准测试中性能提升超10%，并在真实实验中减少了人工干预次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖LiDAR或相机构建的度量地图，但深度感知范围有限，导致长距离导航时因丢弃远距离数据而做出次优、短视的决策，因此需要恢复这些丢失的信息。

Method: 利用卫星图像计算图像/位姿对数据集中可行的导航路径集合，以此监督网络训练，直接从第一人称视角图像中提取长距离可穿越前沿，减少了对大量人工演示数据的需求。

Result: 在多种离线基准测试中，该方法相比现有方法性能提升超过10%，并在真实世界实验中减少了人工干预次数。

Conclusion: 该方法通过卫星图像监督学习，有效弥补了传统度量地图的感知范围限制，显著提升了非结构化地形中的长距离自主导航性能。

Abstract: Standard methods for autonomous navigation in unstructured terrain are prone to myopic behaviors in long-horizon scenarios. The use of metric maps built from LiDAR or cameras provides necessary local geometry and semantic information but is strictly limited by depth sensing range. By discarding data beyond the mapping horizon robots suffer from suboptimal, short-sighted decisions. To recover this lost information, we focus on extracting long-range traversability-aware frontiers directly from first-person-view (FPV) images. By leveraging satellite imagery, we compute the set of feasible navigation paths for a dataset of image/pose pairs and use them to supervise our network, reducing the need for extensive human demonstration data. We demonstrate that this approach improves performance in long-range off-road navigation over existing methods by more than 10% in various offline benchmarks and reduces the number of human interventions incurred in a set of real-world experiments. More details can be found at https://theairlab.org/ss_frontiers_iros .

</details>


### [255] [Closing the Loop in Humanoid VLA: Persistent 3D Object Tokens for Verifiable Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18016)
*Peng Ren,Haoyang Ge,Jiang Zhao,Cong Huang,Yukun Shi,Pei Chi,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出POT-VLA框架，通过持续对象标记化（POT）维护3D对象记录并将其转化为对象令牌，使同一对象状态既可用于生成动作又可进行几何谓词验证，在Unitree G1人形机器人上将成功率从39/80提升至71/80，在Being-0对齐任务上达到44/50（超过原报告37/50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期人形机器人操控中，任务对象被移动、接触、遮挡和恢复时，对象状态会存在"对象状态发散"问题：用于条件化全身动作的对象状态与用于判断动作是否达成预期物理关系的状态不一致，导致动作生成与验证脱节。

Method: 提出持续对象标记化（POT），从RGB-D观测中维护角色索引的3D对象记录，并将其转化为对象令牌供全身动作专家使用。同一对象记录既条件化动作生成，又支持几何谓词检查，形成对象状态既可动作又可验证的闭环执行系统。

Result: 在Unitree G1上：POT-VLA在8个真实任务族上从39/80提升至71/80；Being-0对齐服务任务上达到44/50（原报告37/50）；最大提升出现在需要持续3D关系的任务上。

Conclusion: 持久对象中心状态是人形机器人VLA可验证执行的有效抽象，尤其适用于需要维持3D关系的长时操控任务。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are a promising foundation for general robot control, but long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires the robot to treat task objects as persistent physical entities across movement, contact, occlusion, and recovery. We study this problem as object-state divergence: the object state used to condition a whole-body action can differ from the state used to decide whether the action achieved the intended physical relation. We propose \emph{Persistent Object Tokenization} (POT), which maintains role-indexed 3D object records from RGB-D observations and converts them into object tokens for a whole-body action expert. Instantiated as \emph{POT-VLA}, the same object records condition action generation and support geometric predicate checks, yielding a closed-loop execution system in which object state is both actionable and verifiable. On a Unitree G1, POT-VLA improves a matched direct GR00T-N1.7 baseline from 39/80 to 71/80 successes over eight real-world task families. In an external Being-0-aligned reference, POT-VLA achieves 44/50 successes on aligned service tasks, compared with the 37/50 success reported by the Being-0 paper. The largest gains occur on tasks requiring maintained 3D relations, suggesting that persistent object-centered state is a useful abstraction for verifiable humanoid VLA execution.

</details>


### [256] [RoboHarness: Memory-Driven Orchestration of Heterogeneous Robot Policies for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18060)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Zhanguang Zhang,Mark Coates,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHarness是一个统一框架，通过多模态执行记忆和在线证据，实现对异构机器人策略（VLA、RL、TAMP等）的能力感知分解与路由，并利用Memory Bridge稳定策略切换，显著提升零样本长程规划与分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划方法大多基于同质化、预定义且适用性固定的技能，忽略了异构策略间不确定的能力边界和跨策略分布不匹配问题，无法有效编排具有互补优势的异构策略完成长时域机器人任务。

Method: 提出RoboHarness框架，将独立开发的机器人控制系统封装为可复用的智能体技能；利用多模态执行记忆和在线证据刻画策略能力边界，实现能力感知的任务分解与路由；Memory Bridge通过检索下一策略的执行轨迹，估计其分布内状态区域，引导机器人进入该区域，无需联合策略重训练。

Result: 在三个公开基准、500个定制化任务和135个真实机器人实验中，RoboHarness实现了有效的能力感知路由和稳定的策略编排，在零样本长程规划和分布外鲁棒性上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: RoboHarness作为一个通用框架，能兼容VLA、RL、TAMP、导航策略、MPC、世界-动作模型等多种机器人策略，为解决异构策略编排中的不确定能力边界和分布不匹配问题提供有效方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks require diverse capabilities that no single policy can reliably provide. Heterogeneous policies offer complementary strengths, but orchestrating them requires reasoning over uncertain capability boundaries and cross-policy distribution mismatch, which are largely overlooked by existing planning methods built on homogeneous, predefined skills with fixed applicability. We propose RoboHarness, a unified framework that encapsulates independently developed robot control systems as reusable agentic skills. Although instantiated in this work with VLAs, RL policies, and task-and-motion planning (TAMP) systems, RoboHarness is designed as a general framework compatible with a broader range of robot policies, such as navigation policies, model predictive controllers, and world-action models. RoboHarness uses multi-modal execution memory and online evidence to characterize policy capability boundaries for capability-aware decomposition and routing. To stabilize policy handoffs, its Memory Bridge retrieves execution trajectories associated with the next policy, estimates its in-distribution state region, and guides the robot toward that region without joint policy retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, 500 customized tasks, and 135 real-robot experiments demonstrate effective capability-aware routing and stable policy orchestration, yielding substantial improvements in zero-shot long-horizon planning and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [257] [UniETP: Unifying Environments for Generalizable Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18062)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Ziyou Wang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UniETP，一个统一接口，整合了四种常用的具身任务规划模拟器（AI2-THOR、VirtualHome、Habitat、BEHAVIOR），通过标准化观察和动作空间、构建评估系统以及自动生成多难度级别的数据集，来解决现有模拟器碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务规划研究中，不同模拟器使用各自独立的观察格式、动作类型和任务领域，导致模型评估碎片化、训练数据难以规模化，缺乏统一基准来推动通用具身规划能力的发展。

Method: UniETP通过两方面实现：（1）标准化——将四种模拟器统一为一致的观察和动作空间，并构建支持复杂任务目标的评估系统；（2）多样性——在任务逻辑、实例指代、指令理解等多个维度增强任务多样性和复杂度，并自动构建包含不同难度级别的数据集。

Result: 在提出的基准上对近期模型进行了广泛的具身规划能力评估，并分析了性能瓶颈所在。

Conclusion: UniETP通过统一接口有效解决了模拟器碎片化问题，为通用具身规划研究提供了标准化且多样化的评估平台，有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the problem of Embodied Task Planning, where an agent is required to execute a sequence of atomic actions within an interactive environment to complete a user-specified task. Though a variety of simulators and datasets have previously been built for this task, these efforts are largely isolated, with each using its own observation format, action type, and task domain. This fragmentation complicates comprehensive model evaluation and hinders the scalability of training data. As an effort towards generalizable embodied planning, we propose UniETP, a unified interface integrating four commonly-used simulators (AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR). UniETP is characterized by both standardization and diversity. On one hand, it formalizes all the simulators into a consistent observation and action space, and builds an evaluation system to support complicated task goal. On the other hand, it enhances task diversity and complexity across dimensions like task logic, instance grounding, and instruction understanding, constructing a new dataset with varied levels of difficulty in an automatic manner. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are conducted to evaluate the embodied planning capabilities of recent models and analyze the performance bottlenecks. Codes and data will be available at https://github.com/woyut/UniETP .

</details>


### [258] [Technical Design Review of Duke Robotics Club's Oogway & Crush: AUVs for RoboSub 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18075)
*Patrick Zheng,Saagar Arya,Hung Le,Mathew Chu,Nathanael Ren,Niko Weaver,Isabella Chen,Jill Wang,Raine Cheng,Siddharth Kini,Avrick Altmann,Srinath Iyer,Ivan Chen,Ian Suh,Parker Jones,Pierson Jones,Sebastian deSouza,Suhaani Sriram,Suvas Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 杜克机器人俱乐部为RoboSub 2026开发了两款AUV（Oogway和Crush），通过提升机械、电子和软件三个子系统的可靠性，首次尝试覆盖比赛的四大设计目标：运动、视觉、操控和声学追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队希望从过去狭窄的任务范围中扩展，首次挑战RoboSub的全部四个设计目标，同时保持可靠性以满足竞赛策略需求。

Method: 机械上增加推进器并优化外壳设计；电气上修复故障点、升级推进器控制硬件并重新设计声学系统（含定制PCB和高阶滤波器）；软件上改进状态估计、声纳目标检测、视觉任务规划及IVC。

Result: 系统可靠性提升，声学ping检测效果显著改善，能够尝试更广泛的任务集。

Conclusion: 通过持续可靠性投资和测试基础设施改进，团队在有限的试验时间内实现了更自主、更可靠的运行，为RoboSub 2026做好准备。

Abstract: The Duke Robotics Club presents Oogway and Crush, our AUVs for RoboSub 2026. This year's strategy expands on our previously narrowed scope, targeting all four of RoboSub's design goals for the first time: movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking. This expansion is based on sustained reliability investment across all three subsystems. Mechanically, Crush gained two additional thrusters and a CFD-optimized case, providing pitch stability. Electrically, we addressed accumulated failure points by repairing unreliable connections and upgraded thruster control hardware. We also redesigned our acoustics system, adding a new custom PCB with higher-order filters, significantly improving pinger detection reliability. On the software side, improvements to state estimation, sonar-based object detection, vision-driven task planning, and IVC enable more capable and coordinated autonomous runs. Paired with investments in our testing infrastructure to maximize our limited pool time, we can now attempt a broader set of tasks while maintaining the reliability our competition strategy demands.

</details>


### [259] [Importance Sampling and PCA for Finding Failures in Commercial Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18106)
*Hailey Warner,Duncan Eddy,Shreya Parjan,Caroline Cahilly,Harrison Delecki,Matthias Kleinstauber,Chaitanya Shinde,Jerry Lopez,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种稀有故障发现算法（AST和DiFS）在商业自动驾驶卡车堆栈中的应用，并通过PCA分析将发现的故障分类为常见模式，实现了从故障发现到感知层缺陷系统诊断的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀有故障发现方法几乎只在模拟器中使用简单的学术驾驶系统进行验证，缺乏对商业级鲁棒规划器的适用性研究。

Method: 应用自适应压力测试（AST）和扩散基故障采样（DiFS）两种算法于商业自动驾驶卡车系统；引入基于PCA的统计分析对故障进行分类，聚类主成分并反演PCA变换以恢复通用噪声轨迹。

Result: 两种算法均能在传统蒙特卡洛模拟无法触发的合并和切入场景中找到模拟碰撞；PCA分析成功将故障分类为常见模式，且恢复的噪声轨迹能在相同和相似场景复现故障。

Conclusion: 稀有故障发现算法可泛化至商业级自动驾驶系统，且PCA分析方法提供了从故障发现到感知层缺陷系统诊断的有效途径。

Abstract: Methods for discovering rare failures in autonomous systems have so far been demonstrated almost exclusively in simulations with simple, academic driving stacks, leaving open whether they generalize to the more robust planners used in commercial systems. We address this gap by applying two rare-event discovery algorithms to a commercial autonomous trucking stack. Adaptive stress testing (AST) uses reinforcement learning to search for the most likely noise trajectories leading to a simulated collision, while diffusion-based failure sampling (DiFS) trains a denoising diffusion model to sample a diverse set of failures. We show that both algorithms find simulated collisions during merge and cut-in maneuvers where traditional Monte Carlo simulation does not. To make these failures actionable, we introduce a statistical analysis based on principal component analysis (PCA) that classifies failures into common modes and identifies the timesteps that most influence the outcome. We cluster the principal components and invert the PCA transform to recover generalized noise trajectories, and show that these trajectories reproduce failures in identical and similar scenarios. This provides a path from failure discovery to systematic diagnosis of perception-level flaws.

</details>


### [260] [Isaac Sim-to-Real: Reinforcement Learning based Locomotion for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18135)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab的强化学习框架，实现四足机器人Unitree Go1的全身控制策略，在零样本仿真到现实迁移中表现出与内置控制器相当的速度跟踪性能，更强的抗干扰能力，最高线速度2.0 m/s，角速度1.8 rad/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真中训练的策略部署到真实机器人时存在sim-to-real gap（仿真到现实差距），现有方法难以实现零样本迁移和全身鲁棒控制。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab构建强化学习框架，训练全身控制策略，直接零样本迁移至真实Unitree Go1机器人。

Result: 物理硬件实验表明：速度跟踪性能与机器人的内置控制器相当；更强的抗大干扰恢复能力；实现线速度2.0 m/s，角速度1.8 rad/s。

Conclusion: 所提RL框架成功实现了零样本sim-to-real的全身控制策略，在速度和鲁棒性方面均表现优异。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to locomotion have risen in popularity in recent years, showing the capability for complex legged locomotion and whole-body control. Reinforcement learning (RL), the primary learning-based approach for locomotion, often utilizes a high-performance simulation tool, providing a controlled and efficient training and development environment. However, policies that perform well in simulation frequently encounter unexpected challenges when deployed on a physical system, known as the sim-to-real gap. This work presents a robust RL locomotion framework capable of whole-body control. The proposed RL framework utilizes Nvidia's new set of simulation tools, Isaac Sim, and its companion RL framework, Isaac Lab, for training, achieving a zero-shot sim-to-real policy. The performance of our policy is validated on physical hardware using the Unitree Go1, with experimental results showing similar velocity tracking performance to the quadruped's integrated controller, with a greater ability to recover from large disturbances, and achieve linear velocities of 2.0 m/s and angular velocities of 1.8 rad/s.

</details>


### [261] [World Translation: Minimizing Sim-to-Real Gap with Backward Dynamics Extraction and Unpaired Domain Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18154)
*Xinchen Yao,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出World Translation方法，利用模拟器确定但物理不完美与学习模型准确但部分可观测性不足的互补优势，通过反向提取不可观测动力学信息并作为无配对域翻译问题处理，在多个机器人平台上实现了比基线更准确的动力学建模，并在真实Go2四足机器人上验证了策略迁移的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Real-to-sim方法（尤其是学习动力学模型）面临部分可观测性问题：相同观测可能因不可观测因素产生不同转移。现有方法假设能从观测历史恢复这些因素，但在突然接触等观测历史无信息的情况下会失败。

Method: 提出World Translation方法：1) 反向从观测到的转移中提取不可观测动力学信息；2) 将动力学内容保留、域风格迁移的问题建模为无配对域翻译问题；3) 结合模拟器的确定性优势和学习模型的准确性优势。

Result: 在类人、四足和机械臂平台上，World Translation实现了比基线更准确的动力学建模，在不可观测因素无法从观测历史恢复时提升最大。在真实Go2四足机器人上部署验证了改进的策略迁移效果。

Conclusion: 利用模拟与学习的互补特性，通过反向提取不可观测信息并作为域翻译问题处理，能有效解决部分可观测性带来的动力学建模困难，提升sim-to-real策略迁移效果。

Abstract: The gap between simulation and reality remains a fundamental challenge in deploying simulation-trained robotic policies in the real world. Real-to-sim methods narrow this gap from the real side, learning transition dynamics from real data to build a more realistic digital world. Learned dynamics models are their dominant instance. Such methods, however, face a partial observability problem: the same observation may branch to different transitions due to unobservable factors. Existing methods assume these factors can be recovered from observation history. However, this may fail whenever observation history is uninformative, such as a sudden contact event with no prior warning. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{World Translation}, which exploits a complementary strength of simulators and learned dynamics. Simulators are deterministic but physically imperfect, while learned models are accurate but underdetermined under partial observability. Rather than predicting transitions forward from history, we extract the unobservable dynamics information backward from an observed transition, then translate this feature across simulation and reality as an unpaired domain-translation problem that preserves dynamics content while transferring domain style. Experiments across humanoid, quadruped, and manipulator platforms show that our method achieves more accurate dynamics modeling than baselines, with the largest gains when unobservable factors cannot be recovered from observation history. Real-robot deployment on Go2 quadruped confirms improved policy transfer.

</details>


### [262] [Imitation of Arm Gestures by the Semi-Humanoid Robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18197)
*Anastasiya Ihnatovich,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于MediaPipe姿态估计模型和解析几何方法，开发了半人形机器人NICO对人类手臂姿态的模仿系统，通过单目RGB图像计算关节角度并映射到机器人运动，初步实验表明该方法能产生有意义的模仿动作，但在复杂姿态和手腕运动方面存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无缝的人机交互需要机器人具备模仿人类手势的能力，而人形机器人因其拟人特征在此方面具有优势。现有方法在计算效率和实用性上仍有改进空间，因此本文旨在开发一种基于解析几何和预训练姿态估计模型的高效手臂姿态模仿系统。

Method: 使用MediaPipe框架从单目RGB图像中提取人体关键点（包括手臂关节点和手部关键点）的3D坐标；通过解析几何关系从这些坐标计算关节角度；将计算出的角度映射到NICO半人形机器人的电机配置，并按预定义运动序列执行。

Result: 在6名不同身高的参与者上对多种代表性手臂姿态进行初步实验，结果表明该方法能从单目RGB输入产生有意义的模仿动作，但在复杂姿态和手腕相关运动方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: 基于MediaPipe和解析几何的方法能够实现有效的单目RGB手臂姿态模仿，为低成本人机交互提供了可行方案，但未来需要改进对复杂姿态和精细手腕运动的处理能力。

Abstract: Seamless human-robot interaction (HRI) requires a number of perceptual and motor abilities from the robot, one of them being the imitation of human gestures. Humanoid robots have an advantage in HRI thanks to their anthropomorphic features. In this work, we develop a system for imitation of human arm gestures by the semi-humanoid robot NICO based on analytical geometry and a pretrained MediaPipe pose-estimation model. For each input RGB frame, 3D coordinates of relevant human body landmarks, including arm joints and hand keypoints, are obtained using the MediaPipe framework. Joint angles are then computed from these coordinates using derived geometric relations. Finally, the computed angles are properly mapped to NICO's motor configuration and executed in a predefined motion sequence. Preliminary experiments on several representative arm gestures with six participants of different height indicate that the proposed method can produce meaningful imitative motions from monocular RGB input only, while also highlighting limitations in more complex poses and wrist-related movements.

</details>


### [263] [Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18200)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文条件化的安全评判器（safety critic），用于在杂乱室内环境中从扩散规划器生成的轨迹候选集中自适应选择最优路径。该评判器结合了三个互补项：安全项（含间隙预算惩罚与控制障碍函数残差）、效率项（含平滑度惩罚与安全门控绕路比惩罚）以及距离约束匹配项。通过特权ESDF几何训练并蒸馏为仅依赖感知的选择器，在HM3D/MP3D点目标导航中达到最优SR和SPL，并零样本迁移至Unitree G1人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在杂乱室内环境中的失败常源于固定安全裕度的误校准——保守裕度导致绕路和超时，宽松裕度则因感知偏差产生近边界捷径。扩散规划器虽能生成多样化轨迹，但可靠的候选选择仍是瓶颈。

Method: 提出上下文条件化的安全评判器，包含三个互补项：(i) 安全项：间隙预算惩罚+控制障碍函数残差；(ii) 效率项：平滑度惩罚+安全门控绕路比惩罚；(iii) 距离约束匹配项。在仿真中用特权ESDF几何训练，通过两阶段师生蒸馏转为仅依赖感知的选择器。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D的点目标导航任务（含跨数据集迁移）中，该方法在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上均超过扩散、优化和RL强基线。纯仿真训练后直接迁移到Unitree G1人形机器人，无需任务特定调参即可在杂乱室内场景中导航。

Conclusion: 通过可学习的自适应间隙偏好和两阶段蒸馏策略，该方法有效解决了扩散规划器候选轨迹的可靠选择问题，实现了从仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移，在多个指标上达到最优性能。

Abstract: Robots in cluttered indoor spaces often fail not because they cannot generate collision-free paths, but because a fixed safety margin is mis-calibrated: conservative margins cause detours and timeouts, while permissive margins lead to near-boundary shortcuts under perception bias. Diffusion-based planners propose diverse trajectory candidates from egocentric RGB-D, yet reliable selection remains the bottleneck. We propose a context-conditioned safety critic that learns an adaptive clearance preference for ranking diffusion proposals, decomposed into three complementary terms: (i) a safety term with a clearance-budget penalty and a control-barrier-function residual for waypoint- and transition-wise safety, (ii) an efficiency term combining a smoothness penalty with a safety-gated detour-ratio penalty that avoids detours without incentivizing risky shortcuts, and (iii) a distance-constraint matching term that anchors the learned budget to realized ESDF clearances to prevent margin collapse. We train the critic with privileged ESDF geometry in simulation and distill it into a perception-only selector via a two-stage teacher-student procedure. On PointGoal navigation in HM3D and MP3D, including cross-dataset transfer, our method achieves the highest success rate (SR) and success weighted by path length (SPL) among strong diffusion, optimization, and RL baselines. Trained purely in simulation, it transfers to a Unitree G1 humanoid and navigates cluttered indoor scenes without task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [264] [Optimization of sim-to-real transfer in the humanoid robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18210)
*Juraj Gavura,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于标定的抓取流程，结合YOLO检测、立体视觉校准和视觉反馈，使仿人机器人在不使用RGB-D相机或外部追踪系统的情况下，利用低分辨率鱼眼摄像头实现桌面物体抓取。非线性校准模型在校准区域内表现最佳，而视觉反馈模型在整体工作空间中抓取成功率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仿真到现实迁移中的定位误差问题，避免依赖昂贵的RGB-D相机或外部追踪系统，实现低成本、高精度的机器人抓取。

Method: 在前期2D触觉校准方法基础上，扩展至3D桌面抓取：采用YOLO进行物体和手部检测，利用机器人内置低分辨率鱼眼摄像头进行立体视觉定位，并加入任务特定的抓取校正及视觉反馈模型。

Result: 完全非线性校准模型在校准区域内性能最优；视觉反馈模型在整个桌面工作空间内抓取成功率最高。

Conclusion: 所提标定结合视觉反馈的抓取流程有效缩小了sim-to-real差距，在低成本硬件条件下显著提升了仿人机器人的抓取成功率。

Abstract: Robotic grasping requires accurate coordination between visual perception, object localization, inverse kinematics, and hand control. However, when movements planned in simulation are executed on a physical robot, the sim-to-real gap can cause small positioning errors that prevent successful grasping. In our previous work, we introduced a low-cost haptic calibration method that improved 2D reaching accuracy of the humanoid robot NICO. In this paper, we extend this approach from reaching to tabletop object grasping by adding YOLO-based object and hand detection, stereo vision-based localization using the robot's built-in low-resolution fisheye cameras, and task-specific corrections for grasp execution. Together, these components form a novel calibration-based grasping pipeline that does not require RGB-D cameras, motion capture, or external tracking systems. We also implemented a visual feedback model that aligns the robot hand with the detected object before grasping. Our results show that the fully nonlinear calibration model achieved the best performance inside the calibrated area, while the visual feedback model achieved the highest overall grasping success across the full tabletop workspace.

</details>


### [265] [FM-VLA: Force-based Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18231)
*Ruicheng Li,Qixiu Li,Ruichun Ma,Yu Deng,Lin Luo,Zhiying Du,Jianfeng Xiang,Huizhi Liang,Ruicheng Wang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FM-VLA通过将力历史编码为紧凑的力记忆令牌，使VLA模型能够推理接触丰富的非马尔可夫操作任务，在三个记忆依赖任务中取得80%以上的成功率，显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖视觉记忆来处理非马尔可夫任务，但视觉记忆计算成本高，且在视觉模糊的时间事件（如多次按压按钮）中表现受限。因此需要一种更高效的记忆机制来捕捉接触事件历史。

Method: 使用变分自编码器（VAE）对力历史进行预训练编码，生成紧凑的力记忆令牌；将力潜在表示与短期状态历史作为额外条件令牌注入动作专家模块，使VLA能利用累积的接触事件历史指导操作。

Result: 在找隐藏块、按压按钮、擦拭盘子（特定次数）三个记忆依赖任务中，FM-VLA达到80%以上成功率，且推理开销极小，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于力的记忆是一种高效且有效的非马尔可夫操作推理方案，尤其适用于视觉模糊的接触丰富任务，为VLA模型提供了超越纯视觉记忆的新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive generalization in robotic manipulation, and recent memory-augmented VLAs have relaxed the Markovian assumption by conditioning on past images or language summaries. Vision-based memory approaches address this by conditioning on sampled past image frames, but they are computationally expensive and fundamentally limited when temporal events are visually ambiguous, e.g., pushing a button multiple times with small movements. We propose FM-VLA, a VLA model with force-based memory, enabling temporal context reasoning for non-Markovian, contact-rich manipulation. We encode force histories into compact force memory tokens with a variational autoencoder (VAE) pretrained with force time series reconstruction. By projecting force latent representations and short state history as additional conditioning tokens to the action expert module, we enable VLAs to leverage accumulated contact event history to guide manipulation. We evaluate FM-VLA on three memory-dependent tasks, including finding a hidden block, pressing a button, and wiping a dish for a specific number of times. Our lightweight force memory achieves over 80% success rate with minimal inference overhead, significantly outperforming baseline approaches. Project page: https://qft-333.github.io/FM-VLA-Page/

</details>


### [266] [Patch Policy: Efficient Embodied Control via Dense Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18236)
*Gaoyue Zhou,Zichen Jeff Cui,Ada Langford,Bowen Tan,Yann LeCun,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Patch Policy，一种轻量级架构扩展，使基于Transformer的策略能够直接使用预训练ViT的密集patch特征，无需完整VLM的计算开销。该方法在多个仿真和真实世界环境中，比使用全局池化表示的策略提升40%，比微调后的OpenVLA-OFT提升18%，而参数量仅为后者的0.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略要么将观测压缩为单个全局token丢失空间细节，要么从头训练视觉骨干无法利用大规模预训练优势；而使用密集patch特征的大视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）又过于庞大缓慢。作者希望填补这一空白，在保持轻量高效的同时利用预训练ViT的细粒度空间特征。

Method: 提出Patch Policy，核心是块因果注意力掩码（block-causal attention mask），在保持标准策略时间因果性的同时，允许模型在每个观测上关注多个patch token以及其他状态信息。该架构是对现有基于Transformer策略的最小化扩展，直接消耗预训练的密集patch token，无需完整VLM骨干。

Result: 在4个模拟环境和3个真实世界环境套件中，Patch Policy相比使用全局池化表示的策略取得40%的相对提升，超越微调后的OpenVLA-OFT 18%，而参数量仅为其0.7%。

Conclusion: Patch Policy为机器人社区提供了一种轻量高效的方案，能够直接利用视觉表示学习的持续进步，同时不牺牲高频反应式控制所需的训练效率和推理速度。

Abstract: Pretrained dense visual features from Vision Transformers (ViTs) are powerful yet have been underutilized in robot learning. Modern robot policies either compress each observation into a single global token, or rely on visual backbones trained from scratch, sacrificing both fine-grained spatial detail and the benefits of large-scale visual pre-training. While there exist policies that do operate on dense patch features like large vision-language-action models (VLAs), they tend to be heavy and slow, inheriting the full cost of a billion-parameter vision-language model (VLM) backbone. We close this gap with Patch Policy, a minimal architectural extension that enables transformer-based policies to consume dense pre-trained patch tokens directly without the computational overhead of a full VLM. At its core is a block-causal attention mask that preserves the temporal causality of standard policies while letting the model attend over many patch tokens per observation, alongside other state information. Patch Policy is lightweight, fast, and highly effective. Across four simulated and three real-world environment suites, our method achieves a 40% relative improvement over policies using state-of-the-art global-pooled representations. Furthermore, it surpasses fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT by 18% while using roughly 0.7% of the parameters. We believe Patch Policy provides a pipeline for the robotics community to readily leverage continuing progress in visual representation learning, without sacrificing the training efficiency or inference speed required for high-frequency, reactive control. Videos can be viewed at https://patch-policy.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva,Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLHF中的偏好标签可能受到标注者状态（如压力、情绪）影响，形成一种结构化混淆偏差，并提出了可验证的审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF研究主要关注偏好标签中的随机噪声或标注者间分歧，但忽略了标注者情绪状态随时间变化对标注结果产生的系统性影响。这种结构化偏差可能通过奖励模型传播到策略优化中，导致模型产生偏向性。

Method: 定义了标注者状态漂移、标注者状态混淆和相关标注者状态偏差三个概念；提出"生存级情感真实性"作为可测量的响应模式，涉及词汇、语用、话语和安全性特征；推导了五个可证伪的预测和效应量阈值；设计了审计协议和试点研究计划。

Result: 识别了一种新的结构化偏差来源（标注者状态漂移），建立了理论框架和可操作审计方案，但未对具体模型进行实证推断。

Conclusion: 标注者状态漂移是RLHF偏好数据中一种合理且可测试的结构化偏差来源，需通过系统审计加以识别和缓解。

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [268] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight 1D CNN for Affective Touch Classification in Soft Plush Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16196)
*Aleksandrs Vališevskis,Aleksandrs Okss,Inese Tīģere,Aleksejs Kataševs,Dina Bethere,Anete Hofmane,Airisa Šteinberga,Undīne Gavriļenko,Santa Meļķe,Lucie Matoušková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于MATLAB的开源框架，用于在软体交互伴侣中开发轻量级深度学习模型进行情感触觉识别。通过构建包含1326个标注手势序列的公开数据集，并系统探索468个CNN模型，发现13.2k参数的紧凑型一维CNN（1D CNN）达到75%测试准确率和85%留一法交叉验证准确率。结合启发式阈值滤波与CNN细粒度分类的混合推理流水线被提出作为嵌入式部署策略，实现了在低功耗微控制器上的实时情感触觉识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体传感器化伴侣虽然提供物理安全和情感直觉的交互界面，但其可变形性和多通道触觉传感特性使得从触觉信号中稳健解读人类情感变得复杂。现有方法难以在计算资源有限的嵌入式平台上实现高效、准确的情感触觉识别。

Method: 1) 构建FAIR兼容的公开数据集（1326个标注手势序列，涵盖25名儿童、青少年和成人参与者）；2) 系统探索468个CNN模型的架构和超参数；3) 识别紧凑型膨胀一维CNN（1D CNN）作为最优方案（13.2k参数）；4) 提出混合推理流水线：启发式阈值滤波（处理高强度负面交互）+ CNN细粒度分类（识别微妙社交触觉）。

Result: 13.2k参数的1D CNN模型达到75%测试准确率和85%留一法交叉验证准确率；理论推理分析显示量化部署仅需3.2 MMAC/窗口，支持在目标微控制器上20 Hz实时运行；PC实时仿真证明CNN能检测到传统启发式系统无法识别的微妙社交触觉。

Conclusion: 情感有意义的、隐私保护的触觉解释在计算上可行，可直接嵌入软体治疗伴侣中。混合推理流水线（启发式滤波+CNN细分类）被提出作为嵌入式部署策略，硬件集成将在后续研究中完成。

Abstract: Soft, sensorized companions offer a physically safe and emotionally intuitive interface for socially assistive technologies, yet their deformability and multichannel tactile sensing complicate the robust interpretation of human affect. This study presents a complete open-source MATLAB-based framework for the development and validation of compact deep learning models for affective touch recognition in soft interactive companions. As a primary contribution, a diverse FAIR-compliant dataset of 1326 labelled gesture sequences collected from 25 participants spanning children, teenagers, and adults is made publicly available, providing a reusable resource for future research in affective touch recognition. Through systematic architecture and hyperparameter exploration across 468 CNN models, the study identifies compact dilated one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D CNNs) as the most effective solution, with a 13.2k-parameter model achieving 75% test accuracy and 85% mean leave-one-subject-out cross-validation accuracy. Theoretical inference-time analysis shows that quantized deployment requires 3.2 MMAC per window, compatible with 20 Hz real-time operation on the target microcontroller. PC-based real-time simulation with the physical toy streaming sensor data demonstrates that the CNN resolves subtle social touches that the previous heuristic system failed to detect, whereas high-force negative interactions are captured more reliably by trivial threshold-based logic. The resulting hybrid inference pipeline - instantaneous heuristic filtering followed by CNN-based nuanced gesture classification - is proposed as the embedded deployment strategy. The study demonstrates that emotionally meaningful, privacy-preserving touch interpretation is computationally feasible for direct embedding within soft therapeutic companions, with hardware integration addressed in a forthcoming study.

</details>


### [269] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个跨领域框架，测试了六个大型语言模型和100名人类参与者在空间导航、临床分诊和金融分配任务中的风险态度。研究发现，大多数LLM表现出任务内一致性、跨领域排序稳定性和相对人类更受限的风险态度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放、高风险的AI部署场景中，关键维度——感知风险如何转化为行动——尚未被衡量。作者希望测试LLM是否表现出系统且一致的风险态度。

Method: 提出跨领域框架，将上下文风险信念与类别决策解耦，应用于六个LLM和100名人类参与者的三项任务（空间导航、临床分诊、金融分配）。使用回归模型提取信念到决策的映射，量化风险敏感度和风险态度偏差。

Result: 大多数LLM表现出：(i) 任务内一致性；(ii) 跨领域排序稳定性；(iii) 相比人类基线，风险态度分布更受限。

Conclusion: 风险态度是LLM行为中稳定且此前未被量化的维度，为评估和对齐AI系统的开放决策奠定了基础，并激励进一步探索这些内在行为倾向的起源。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [270] [A Survey on GNN-based Link Prediction: Techniques, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16198)
*Chengcheng Sun,Yajie Song,Cheng Zhai,Jiayun Tian,Jia Yang,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Zhixiao Wang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于GNN的链接预测方法，从技术和应用两个维度提出了创新分类法，涵盖GCN、GAE、GAT和GFormer等编码器架构，并探讨了知识图谱和推荐系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述缺乏对底层GNN架构和多样化图结构的系统性探索，存在关键研究空白，亟需从GNN视角对链接预测进行全面梳理。

Method: 提出创新分类法：(1)技术视角——按GCN、GAE、GAT、GFormer等编码器架构分类；(2)应用视角——聚焦知识图谱和推荐系统中的链接预测用例。

Result: 系统梳理了各类GNN架构在链接预测中的优缺点，总结了在知识图谱和推荐系统中的应用现状。

Conclusion: 指出了当前挑战并讨论了有前景的未来方向。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as the leading paradigm for link prediction, enabling the inference of missing connections and the anticipation of potential future links. However, existing reviews lack systematic exploration specifically targeting underlying GNN architectures and diverse graph structures. To address this critical gap, this paper provides a comprehensive review of GNN-based link prediction from a novel and dedicated GNN perspective. We propose an innovative taxonomy that categorizes recent advancements based on techniques and applications. From a technique perspective, we focus on key GNN encoder architectures, including GCN-based, GAE-based, GAT-based, and GFormer-based methods, discussing their strengths and limitations. From an application perspective, we highlight prominent use cases of link prediction in knowledge graphs and recommendation systems, demonstrating their real-world impact. In addition, we examine the current challenges and discuss promising future directions.

</details>


### [271] [PlanFlip: Attacking Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Planning-Phase Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16199)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种针对多智能体LLM系统中Planner阶段的提示注入攻击，在9个前沿模型上测试发现：模型越强越易受攻击（GPT-5 ASR=0.68）、同质化管道存在盲点、推理增强模型（DeepSeek-R1）可抵抗攻击。并提出两种检测方法（D1和D2），核心见解是异构模型多样性是多智能体系统的安全前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统越来越依赖Planner将目标分解为子任务序列，但现有研究忽略了规划阶段作为关键攻击面的风险——一次注入Planner的上下文就能实现级联放大，同时污染所有下游子任务。作者旨在揭示这一漏洞并提出防御方案。

Method: 提出PlanFlip框架，包含四种规划阶段提示注入攻击：GoalSubstitution（PF-1，目标替换）、PriorityInversion（PF-2，优先级反转）、ContextPollution（PF-3，上下文污染）和RoleConfusion（PF-4，角色混淆）。攻击伪装成合理的工具输出以绕过关键词过滤器。在9个前沿LLM上进行了3,479轮实验评估。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)模型能力放大了脆弱性——GPT-5攻击成功率最高（ASR=0.68），与"越强越安全"的假设相悖；(2)同质化管道存在相关智能体盲点——GPT-4o和Llama-3.3-70B的ASR接近0但Stealth=1.00且StepShift>0，攻击重构了计划而相同骨干网络的Critic报告一致性；(3)推理增强模型抵抗注入——DeepSeek-R1在所有攻击下StepShift=0.00。提出的GoalAnchorCheck（D1）和CrossAgentConsensus（D2）检测率达1.00，在16个单元格中的15个优于同骨干基线。

Conclusion: 异构模型多样性是多智能体系统安全的前提条件；同质骨干网络内的冗余对规划阶段攻击不提供任何保护。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly rely on a Planner to decompose goals into sub-task sequences that downstream Executor and Critic agents execute and audit. We identify the planning phase as a critical attack surface: a single injection into the Planner's context achieves cascade amplification, corrupting all downstream sub-tasks simultaneously. We introduce PlanFlip, a framework comprising four planning-phase prompt injection attacks -- GoalSubstitution (PF-1), PriorityInversion (PF-2), ContextPollution (PF-3), and RoleConfusion (PF-4) -- each disguised as plausible tool outputs to evade keyword filters. Evaluating nine frontier LLMs across 3,479 episodes, we uncover three findings: (1) capability amplifies vulnerability -- GPT-5 achieves the highest attack success rate (ASR = 0.68), contradicting the assumption that stronger models are inherently more secure; (2) homogeneous pipelines exhibit a correlated-agent blind spot -- GPT-4o and Llama-3.3-70B show ASR near 0 yet Stealth = 1.00 and StepShift > 0, with attacks restructuring plans while the same-backbone Critic reports alignment (two independent judges confirm -0.20 to -0.32 semantic deviation, r = 0.943); (3) reasoning-augmented models resist injections -- DeepSeek-R1 achieves StepShift = 0.00 across all attacks. We propose GoalAnchorCheck (D1) and CrossAgentConsensus (D2), achieving detection rates up to 1.00 and outperforming same-backbone baselines in 15 of 16 cells. Our key insight: heterogeneous model diversity is a security prerequisite for multi-agent systems; redundancy within a homogeneous backbone provides no protection against planning-phase attacks.

</details>


### [272] [Deterministic Replay for AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16200)
*Rasheed Mudasiru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 agrepl，一个开发者优先的CLI框架，通过MITM代理拦截所有外部交互，实现AI agent执行的可确定性重放。实验表明重放保真度F=1.0，每步延迟中位数降低98.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent系统（LLM+外部工具/API）本质是非确定性的，LLM采样方差、外部API状态、CDN基础设施头信息、执行环境噪声等因素导致无法忠实地重放先前agent运行。现有可观测平台虽能捕获执行日志，但无法在隔离环境中复现运行过程。

Method: 提出agrepl框架：(1) 在传输层通过MITM代理拦截所有外部交互；(2) 序列化为结构化执行轨迹；(3) 在严格隔离（零出站网络访问）环境中重放。形式化定义agent执行模型、请求-键匹配函数K(s)，证明确定性不变性。提出噪声感知diff算法，将HTTP头差异分类为信号层和噪声层。

Result: 在五个工作负载上（n=250个重放实例）进行实证评估，重放保真度F=1.0，每步延迟中位数降低98.3%。

Conclusion: agrepl成功解决了AI agent系统的确定性重放问题，用Go实现，作为单个静态二进制文件发布，采用MIT许可证。为LLM调试和可复现性提供了实用工具。

Abstract: AI agent systems that couple large language models (LLMs) with external tools and APIs are inherently non-deterministic: LLM sampling variance, external API state, CDN infrastructure headers, and execution-environment noise collectively prevent any prior agent run from being faithfully re-executed. Existing observability platforms capture execution logs but cannot reproduce a run in isolation. We present agrepl, a developer-first CLI framework for deterministic replay of agent executions. agrepl intercepts all external interactions at the transport layer via a man-in-the-middle (MITM) proxy, serialises them as structured execution traces, and replays them in a strictly isolated environment with zero outbound network access. We formalise the agent execution model, define the request-key matching function K(s), and prove the determinism invariant. We introduce a noise-aware diff algorithm classifying HTTP header divergence into signal and noise tiers. Empirical evaluation across five workloads (n = 250 replay instances) demonstrates replay fidelity F = 1.0 and a median per-step latency reduction of 98.3%. agrepl is implemented in Go, ships as a single static binary, and is released under the MIT licence.
  Keywords: AI agents, deterministic replay, LLM debugging, reproducibility, MITM proxy, execution tracing, record/replay systems.

</details>


### [273] [Generative Ontology Induction: Domain-Agnostic Schema Discovery from Document Corpora Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16201)
*Sergei Sergienko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Generative Ontology Induction (GOI)，一种领域无关的框架，能从示例语料中诱导出生成式蓝图（实体、维度、属性、关系、约束），并导出为类型化图（六种节点、七种边）。在四个对比本体上的验证表明，GOI提示生成的结构覆盖率达95-100%，而通用模板覆盖率在52.2%-97.8%之间波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体工程仍是知识密集型AI系统的关键瓶颈。现有自动化方法要么依赖预定义模式，要么局限于窄领域，要么产生不适合下游管道的非结构化输出。

Method: GOI是一个领域无关框架，从示例语料中诱导出生成式蓝图（包含实体、维度、属性、关系和约束），并将其导出为YAML/JSON格式的类型化图（六种节点类型、七种边类型）。同时提出Node Coverage Score这一新评估指标，衡量生成输出中结构本体节点（类、属性、维度）的覆盖率。

Result: 在四个对比本体上的受控生成验证表明：GOI提示生成覆盖了每个案例95-100%的结构骨干；而通用三字段模板在发票模式上达到97.8%，但在职位描述本体上降至52.2%，疼痛管理本体上62.2%，专业服务合同本体上78.3%。结构覆盖率不受文档类型对模型熟悉程度的影响。

Conclusion: GOI在跨领域的本体诱导中表现出稳定且优异的结构覆盖率（95-100%），显著优于通用模板方法，且不依赖于模型对文档类型的先验熟悉度，为知识密集型AI系统中的本体工程提供了一种有效的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Ontology engineering remains a critical bottleneck in knowledge-intensive AI systems. Existing automated approaches either depend on predefined schemas, operate within narrow domains, or produce unstructured outputs unsuitable for downstream pipelines.
  We introduce Generative Ontology Induction (GOI), a domain-agnostic framework that induces a generative blueprint - entities, dimensions, properties, relationships, and constraints - from a corpus of examples and exports it as a typed graph (six node types, seven edge types) in YAML/JSON. We introduce the Node Coverage Score, a novel evaluation metric that measures the fraction of structural ontology nodes (classes, properties, and dimensions) appearing in generated outputs.
  A controlled generative validation on four contrasting ontologies - a familiar Software Services Invoice schema, a custom Job Description Ontology, a confidential Pain-Management Clinical Visit Record Ontology, and a Professional Services Contract & Statement of Work Ontology - shows that GOI-prompted generation covers 95-100% of the structural backbone in every case; a generic three-field template holds at 97.8% on the invoice schema but drops to 52.2% on the Job Description Ontology, 62.2% on the Pain-Management ontology, and 78.3% on the Professional Services Contract ontology. The structural coverage holds regardless of how familiar the document type is to the model.

</details>


### [274] [Democratizing AI with Small Language Models: Structured Benchmarking and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Local Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16202)
*Daniel Cersosimo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个1085示例、16主题的多选题基准，系统评估了9个开源小模型（135M-3B参数）在结构化本地部署场景下的能力。研究表明，通过低成本的参数高效微调（4-bit NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA），小模型可以在特定领域达到与更大模型相当的性能，验证了AI民主化的可行路径不在于追求通用大模型，而在于在硬件和治理约束下实现对可审计、可专业化的小模型的理性选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI民主化讨论过度聚焦于模型规模和通用能力，忽视了大多数机构在硬件预算、治理合规、审计需求等方面的实际约束。本文旨在回答一个关键问题：在普通机构可承受的硬件（如NVIDIA L4级GPU）和治理条件下，小模型能否通过系统化的基准测试和低成本微调，成为可部署的本地专家系统。

Method: 1) 构建了一个包含1085个示例、覆盖16个主题的多选题基准，强调符号精确性、格式化约束、信息提取和短期语义决策（严格单字母输出协议）；2) 对9个135M-3B参数的开源权重模型进行零样本评估；3) 采用共享的参数高效微调流程：4-bit NF4量化+DoRA/LoRA适配器，在NVIDIA L4级GPU上运行；4) 在108示例的保留集上评估微调效果。

Result: 基座评估中：Qwen Coder 3B以75.67%的严格准确率领先，Qwen2.5 1.5B (67.10%)、Qwen3.5 2B (64.98%)、Granite 3.3 2B (64.61%)紧随其后。微调后：Qwen Coder 3B提升+26.85个百分点，SmolLM2 1.7B提升+25.92，Qwen2.5 1.5B提升+19.44，SmolLM2 360M提升+10.18，SmolLM2 135M提升+5.55。

Conclusion: 在排序、主题异质性、难度分层、错误构成、效率前沿和主题条件迁移等多个维度上，结论一致：通过基准构建、跨模型评估和低成本专业化的系统化工作流程，部分3B以下的小模型已经可以作为结构化小众工作负载的本地专家模型部署，这为AI民主化提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: AI democratization is not primarily a question of matching frontier-scale generality; it is a question of whether capable models can be selected, audited, and specialized under hardware and governance constraints that ordinary institutions can actually satisfy. This paper studies that problem through a controlled evaluation of nine open-weight language models between 135M and 3B parameters on a 1,085-example, 16-topic multiple-choice benchmark designed for structured local deployment. The benchmark emphasizes symbolic precision, constrained formatting, extraction, and short-horizon semantic decision making under a strict one-letter output protocol. A shared parameter-efficient fine-tuning pipeline then adapts a subset of models using 4-bit NF4 quantization with DoRA/LoRA-style adapters on an NVIDIA L4-class budget. In base evaluation, Qwen Coder 3B leads at 75.67% strict accuracy, followed by Qwen2.5 1.5B at 67.10%, Qwen3.5 2B at 64.98%, and Granite 3.3 2B at 64.61%. On the shared 108-example held-out fine-tuning split, adaptation improves Qwen Coder 3B by +26.85 points, SmolLM2 1.7B by +25.92, Qwen2.5 1.5B by +19.44, SmolLM2 360M by +10.18, and SmolLM2 135M by +5.55. Across ranking, topic-level heterogeneity, difficulty strata, failure composition, efficiency frontiers, and topic-conditioned transfer, the same conclusion recurs: a disciplined workflow of benchmark construction, cross-model evaluation, and low-cost specialization already makes a subset of sub-3B models viable as local experts for structured niche workloads.

</details>


### [275] [Masked Diffusion Language Models are Strong and Steerable Text-Based World Models for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16204)
*Darshan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的文本世界建模方法，通过双向锚点感知去噪解决自回归世界模型在全局状态锚点（如工具模式、先验轮次、预期结果）上的条件偏差问题。构建239,403条状态-动作轨迹数据集，在9个开源环境和12个前沿模型家族上验证，MDLM以1/4参数量实现更优的连贯性、基础性和 rollout 多样性。引入即插即用的GRPO训练框架，在三个OOD环境零样本迁移中实现最高47%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习需要多样化训练环境，但手工设计环境随模型能力提升失效；稀疏奖励和长程任务导致模式坍缩；自回归世界模型存在从左到右的偏差，无法以全局状态锚点为条件进行可控生成。

Method: (1) 形式化文本世界建模为可控状态转移动态问题，分解为初始状态、任务上下文、工具模式、领域规则和导向指令；(2) 构建239,403条状态-动作轨迹数据集；(3) 比较自回归LM与掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM），利用双向锚点感知去噪；(4) 提出即插即用的GRPO训练框架，含确定性状态检查；(5) 在三个OOD环境进行零样本迁移消融实验。

Result: MDLM以1/4参数量超越自回归LLM，实现更优的连贯性、基础性和 rollout 多样性，推理延迟相当。在ScienceWorld、ALFWorld、AppWorld三个OOD环境中，基于LFM2.5/Qwen3/Mistral（1.2B-7B）的零样本迁移实现最高47%绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散语言模型是自回归世界模型的有效替代方案，尤其适合需要全局锚点条件化的文本世界建模；开源的GRPO训练框架和数据集为该方向提供了有力基准。

Abstract: Recent growth in reinforcement learning (RL) has surfaced a need for diverse, specialized training environments. Hand-curated environments with fixed task and reward difficulties become ineffective signals as model performance improves, and sparse rewards over long horizons induce mode collapse on specific workflows or tool structures. World models that simulate environment states have matched pure rollout performance, making them promising for scaling diversity on-demand. However, autoregressive (AR) world models suffer from a left-to-right bias preventing conditioning on globally interdependent state anchors such as tool schemas, prior turns, and expected outcomes. We (i) formalize text-based world modeling as a steerable transition-dynamics problem decomposed into initial state, task context, tool schemas, domain rules, and steering directives, and (ii) curate 239,403 grounded state-action trajectories spanning nine open-source environments and twelve frontier model families. We compare AR LMs and masked diffusion language models (MDLMs), showing MDLMs, via bidirectional anchor-aware denoising, achieve better coherence, groundedness, and empirically validated rollout diversity than LLMs over 4x their parameter size, at comparable inference latency. We introduce a plug-and-play GRPO training framework with deterministic state checks, and perform zero-shot transfer ablations on three OOD environments (ScienceWorld, ALFWorld, AppWorld) across three 1.2B-7B agent backbones (LFM2.5, Qwen3, Mistral), achieving up to 47% absolute gains over baselines without environment-specific fine-tuning. We further conduct behavioral analysis of failure modes under adversarial scenarios and human evaluation on realism, outcome correctness, and training utility. We open-source our work to encourage research in this direction.

</details>


### [276] [It Takes 8 Tokens: Weak-to-Strong Off-Policy RL via Auxiliary Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16205)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Liwei Qian,Xin Pei,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对强化学习奖励验证范式中模型自生成样本语义冗余、陷入相同"推理盆地"的问题，提出W2SPO方法——利用弱辅助模型注入短令牌片段以扩展探索空间，在4B规模数学推理基准上超越基线，实现64.2% Pass@1和3.55倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习奖励验证范式存在"支持受限瓶颈"：在困难推理任务上，目标模型自生成样本存在语义冗余，收敛到相同的错误"推理盆地"，导致奖励对比信号微弱，无法有效更新策略。

Method: 提出W2SPO，一种离线策略RL方法。利用计算效率更高的弱辅助模型生成短片段（通常仅8个令牌）注入到目标模型中间轨迹中，目标模型从这些偏离状态继续完成推理路径。策略更新仅基于最终可验证奖励作用于这些短插入片段。

Result: 在4B规模模型上，W2SPO在数学推理基准中超越评估的后训练基线。在相同采样预算下，与原始GRPO相比，Pass@1从62.3%提升至64.2%，训练速度提升3.55倍。

Conclusion: 弱辅助分支可以通过扩展局部探索支持来诱导更强的目标推理策略，验证了"弱到强"学习范式在缓解奖励稀疏与探索不足问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has emerged as a standard approach for enhancing reasoning in large language models, which typically optimizes the policy by contrasting multiple self generated rollouts. However, we identify a critical support limited bottleneck in this paradigm: on challenging reasoning tasks, the target model's samples often exhibit semantic redundancy, converging into the same erroneous "reasoning basins" that offer negligible reward contrast for policy updates. In this paper, we propose to overcome this limitation through a weak to strong learning paradigm, where a policy's exploration is informed by a weaker but computationally efficient auxiliary model. We introduce W2SPO, an off policy RL method that injects short auxiliary segments often as brief as 8 tokens into intermediate target model trajectories and the target model then completes the reasoning path from these diverted states. Policy updates are restricted to these short inserted segments based on final verifiable rewards. Empirically, W2SPO achieves superior performance among evaluated 4B scale models on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, outperforming evaluated post trained baselines. Compared with vanilla GRPO under the same sampling budget, W2SPO improves Pass@1 from 62.3% to 64.2% while achieving a 3.55 times training speedup. These results suggest that weak auxiliary branches can induce stronger target reasoning policies by expanding local exploration support.

</details>


### [277] [PPO-HSC: An Exploratory Reinforcement Learning Framework Based on Wide-Area Policy Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16206)
*Yujie Shen,Haowen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPO-HSC通过在标准RLVR中引入高阶采样覆盖奖励，激励LLM发现低相似度高有效性的推理模式，从而解决微调中的模式坍缩问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上提升了解决方案多样性和状态空间覆盖，同时保持或超越了现有RL基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR虽然能有效强化高奖励轨迹，但会导致模型过度优化已知解决方案，丧失好奇心和探索更广阔解空间的能力，导致模式坍缩（即"无形枷锁"）。

Method: PPO-HSC框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 高阶采样覆盖（HSC）奖励，激励发现"低相似性高有效性"的推理模式；(2) 动态轨迹库，维护已验证的独特解决方案；(3) 似然约束，确保结构合理性。通过这些机制提供可微分的信号来奖励语义新颖性。

Result: 在GSM8K、SVAMP数学推理和代码生成任务上的实验表明，PPO-HSC显著提升了解决方案多样性和状态空间覆盖率，同时保持或超越了现有SOTA RL基线的准确率和语法完整性。

Conclusion: PPO-HSC有效解决了LLM微调中的模式坍缩问题，通过鼓励探索多样化但有效的解决方案，在保持性能的同时提升了模型的探索能力和泛化潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces PPO-HSC (Proximal Policy Optimization with High-order Sampling Coverage), an exploratory reinforcement learning framework designed to address the "Invisible Shackles" of mode collapse in Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning. While standard Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) effectively reinforces high-reward trajectories, it often leads models to over-optimize known solutions, sacrificing curiosity and the ability to explore broader solution manifolds. To overcome this, PPO-HSC incorporates a High-order Sampling Coverage (HSC) reward that incentivizes the discovery of "low-similarity yet high-validity" reasoning patterns. By maintaining a dynamic trajectory library of verified unique solutions, the framework provides a differentiable signal that rewards semantic novelty while ensuring structural rationality through a plausibility constraint. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning (GSM8K, SVAMP) and code generation tasks demonstrate that PPO-HSC significantly enhances solution diversity and state-space coverage while maintaining or surpassing the accuracy and syntax integrity of state-of-the-art RL baselines.

</details>


### [278] [JUMP: Single-Pass Membership Inference on Fine-Tuned Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16207)
*Yeachan Jun,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JUMP是一种针对离散扩散语言模型(dLLM)的成员推断攻击方法，利用dLLM的任意顺序解码和并行解码特性，通过联合不确定性引导的掩码探测，在一次评分中完成攻击，相较于SAMA方法将ROC-AUC从0.82提升至0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对离散扩散语言模型的成员推断攻击方法SAMA虽然利用了任意顺序接口，但仅将其作为随机化手段，需要大量目标/参考模型查询，未能充分利用dLLM的并行解码特性，效率低下。

Method: JUMP包含两个阶段：选择阶段利用任意顺序解码能力，选择参考模型置信度低的位置；评分阶段利用并行解码能力，将所有选定位置通过一次联合掩码查询同时评分，计算裁剪后的目标/参考重建差距统计量。

Result: 在微调的LLaDA-8B-Base模型上，跨越六个MIMIR数据集，JUMP将平均ROC-AUC从SAMA的0.82提升至0.90，显著提升了低假阳性率(FPR)下的检测性能，且仅需每个目标/参考模型一次选择阶段和一次评分阶段。

Conclusion: JUMP通过充分利用扩散语言模型的任意顺序和并行解码两大特性，实现了更高效、更准确的成员推断攻击，证明了这两种特性在隐私攻击中的价值。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) test whether a candidate example appeared in a model's training data. We study MIAs for fine-tuned discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs), where membership means inclusion in the target model's fine-tuning set. Unlike autoregressive language models, dLLMs allow an attacker to choose arbitrary mask sets and obtain token distributions for all masked positions in parallel. The prior dLLM attack, SAMA, follows a natural loss-mimicking strategy by averaging reconstruction signals over many randomly sampled masks, but it uses the any-order interface only as randomization and requires many target/reference queries. We propose JUMP (Joint Uncertainty-Guided Mask Probing), a single-pass scoring attack that exploits both distinctive properties of dLLMs: any-order decodability is used to select low-reference-confidence positions, and parallel decodability is used to score all selected positions through one joint masked query per model. JUMP masks the selected positions jointly and computes a clipped target/reference reconstruction-gap statistic. On fine-tuned LLaDA-8B-Base across six MIMIR domains, JUMP improves mean ROC-AUC from 0.82 to 0.90 over SAMA and substantially improves low-FPR detection, while requiring only one selector pass and one scoring pass through each of the target and reference models.

</details>


### [279] [ColGraphRAG: Late-Interaction Evidence Retrieval for Multimodal GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16208)
*Seonok Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文探讨在多模态问答中，用后期交互的多向量评分（ColBERT/ColPali系列）替换传统单向量双编码器，来改进图链接图像的候选排序，从而提升检索和下游QA表现，尤其在视觉证据重要的问题上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图基多模态问答中，图链接图像使用单向量双编码器进行相似度排序，但这种方法忽略了图像内部的patch级和token级结构，导致细粒度对齐不足，限制了端到端准确性。

Method: 在保持离线图构建、文本/表格检索、结构化提取和下游推理不变的前提下，将图链接图像节点的视觉候选排序算子替换为ColBERT/ColPali系列的后期交互MaxSim风格多向量评分方法。

Result: 在MultimodalQA数据集上，该方法改善了检索阶段图链接图像候选的点估计，并带来下游QA准确率的提升；在视觉证据重要的问题上提升更明显，而文本主导问题上趋势混杂。

Conclusion: 实验结果为图链接视觉证据的有效纳入提供了机制层面的证据，但更广泛的验证和更精细的图级别诊断仍是未来重要工作。

Abstract: Graph-grounded multimodal question answering organizes text, tables, and images in a structured evidence graph, yet end-to-end accuracy depends on which multimodal assets are ranked highly enough to enter downstream reasoning; for graph-linked images, single-vector bi-encoder similarity can discard patch- and token-level structure needed for fine-grained alignment. We evaluate replacing the visual candidate-ranking operator over graph-linked image nodes with late-interaction MaxSim-style multi-vector scoring in the ColBERT/ColPali lineage, while keeping offline graph construction, text- and table-side retrieval, structured extraction, and downstream reasoning unchanged. On MultimodalQA, this change is associated with improved retrieval-stage point estimates for graph-linked image candidates and downstream QA gains, with larger movement where visual evidence matters most and mixed trends on text-dominant questions; we interpret the pattern as mechanism-level evidence for graph-linked visual evidence inclusion, while broader validation and finer graph-level diagnostics remain important future work.

</details>


### [280] [Shapley Context Pruning: A Cooperative Game Perspective for Context Reranking and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16209)
*Yanqiao Chen,Dongsheng Hou,Yuhan Rui,Zhen Cao,Yepang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shapley Context Pruning (SCP)，一种基于合作博弈论（Shapley值）的上下文重排序与剪枝框架。采用Deep Sets架构实现句子级排列不变价值函数，利用预训练语言模型作为句子编码器，通过成对边际排序损失优化。结合蒙特卡洛采样保证可扩展性并提供理论误差界，在多项长上下文任务中取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的上下文重排序与剪枝方法多依赖启发式损失函数和经验归因，缺乏可解释且统一的框架。作者希望从合作博弈论角度建立严格的上下文重要性归因机制，解决"哪些上下文片段对生成最重要"这一核心问题。

Method: 1) 将上下文建模为合作博弈，用Shapley值量化每个句子的边际贡献；2) 采用Deep Sets架构近似句子级的排列不变价值函数；3) 使用预训练语言模型作为句子编码器；4) 通过成对边际排序损失优化；5) 利用蒙特卡洛采样实现高效训练与推理，并提供理论误差界限和样本复杂度保证。

Result: 在支持句召回、NIAH评估、长上下文QA和多跳推理等全面实验中，模型在多项下游QA任务上达到与强基线竞争的性能。消融实验验证了嵌入质量和归因策略的有效性。

Conclusion: SCP框架成功将合作博弈论引入上下文重排序，在保持数学严谨性的同时实现实际可扩展性，为RAG系统的上下文优化提供了兼具可解释性和理论保证的新范式。

Abstract: Context reranking and pruning have become essential for improving the efficiency of modern Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems, yet an interpretable and unified framework remains underexplored. Previous work has primarily emphasized lexical retrieval, cross-encoder architectures, model distillation, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), mostly relying on heuristic loss functions and empirical attribution. This paper presents Shapley Context Pruning (SCP), a novel framework for context reranking that establishes a cooperative-game-theory perspective for importance attribution by modeling the context as a cooperative game. Balancing the trade-off between fine-grained and coarse-grained representations, we employ a Deep Sets architecture to approximate a permutation-invariant value function at the sentence level, utilizing pre-trained language models as sentence embedders and optimizing via a pairwise margin ranking loss. To ensure practical scalability without sacrificing mathematical rigor, we leverage Monte-Carlo sampling for efficient training and inference, providing formal theoretical error bounds and sample complexity guarantees for preserving Top-K subset rankings. Furthermore, we conduct comprehensive experiments-spanning supporting-sentence recall, Needle-in-the-Haystack (NIAH) evaluations, long-context QA, and multi-hop reasoning-alongside rigorous ablation studies on embedding quality and attribution strategies. The model achieves competitive downstream QA performance against robust baselines.

</details>


### [281] [A Survey on the Verification of Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16210)
*Luca Marzari,Ezio Bartocci,Enrico Marchesini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇综述为强化学习策略验证方法提供了一个统一的分类视角，沿着验证范式、时间范围和保证强度三个轴对现有方法进行分类，并澄清了它们之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在安全关键领域的应用日益广泛，但基于神经网络的策略缺乏严格的行为保证，这成为部署的主要障碍。随着策略表达能力和规模的增长，该挑战更加突出，导致RL策略验证领域快速发展但概念分散。

Method: 提出一个三维分类法：验证范式（形式化 vs 概率性）、时间范围（单步 vs 多步）和保证强度。在此基础上统一理论基础，明确隐含假设与局限性，并识别新兴方向。

Result: 构建了一个统一的分类框架，澄清了现有RL验证方法之间的关系，使表面分散的研究工作有了系统的组织结构。

Conclusion: 通过提供统一视角、明确假设与局限，为RL策略验证领域建立了更清晰的研究地图，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly applied in complex, safety-critical domains, yet the lack of rigorous behavioral guarantees for neural network-based policies remains a major barrier to deployment.
